//*特别感谢Discord上的Nickles或Buddy的支持，使我能比计划更早地上传这一章。希望大家喜欢。如果我自己这么说的话，这是我最有趣的章节之一。我很享受同时玩弄这么多动态关系。*

* COMMENT Chapter 19: Season 2 Ep 3 (英语原文)

"Okay..." Kenichi was the first to speak as the music died out with
Subaru's exit towards the light and the last appearance of Echidna
played out. "Can someone explain to me... Everything that just happened?
Please?"

He was pleading to the wrong people as everyone in the room dealt with
what they'd seen.

"Apologies, we don't mean to be insensitive to you, Natsuki-san." Otto,
who was hanging his head low after what they'd watched, was the one
trying to show a little diplomacy.

"I didn't blame any of you for being insensitive, man!" Kenichi tried to
stop his frustration from showing in his tone but failed. "Why was there
music blasting over... what we've just seen... like some... some
freaking anime?!"

Many gave confused looks at this question, including nearly everyone in
the Emilia and Crusch camp, while Anastasia looked more interested than
ever by the man's wording.

"... Us mercenaries and civilians dunno what such a thing is..." Ricardo
endeavored to say from the back with a wince, seeing that the parents
weren't really in the mood. "Sorry..."

"How come you ignored it?" Noako asked, her eyes on Emilia and Rem with
a clear need for answers, yet no hint of accusation. "Did this... Has it
happened before? I heard someone say that this was 'another'..."

"Um..." Emilia swallowed, trying to shuffle her hand slowly to Subaru's
sleeping sleeve as she worked up the courage to speak with his mother.
"Y-Yes. Every time one of these 'viewings' begins or ends... We see
these visions with music playing in the back... It's... Unsavory to say
the least."

"Not that the melodies themselves are bad, though..." Felt muttered from
the front, which caused Heinkel to snort from the back.

"Melodies, my fuckin' ass..." Heinkel was bitter as he said this. "Ain't
none of what we've watched so far is deserving of praise. Much less some
fucking weird-ass... musical outlet while the kid's suffering. How many
times has this happened before I was summoned in 'ere with the kid?" He
pointed to Schult while glaring down at the others.

"It's happened since the very beginning, I suppose." Beatrice narrowed
her eyes at the floor, remembering the very first viewing.

The parents exchanged what was the first of many glances at each other
before they moved their eyes to Subaru's sleeping face.

"You don't think..." Kenichi's voice broke slightly as his wife held on
to him tighter. "No... There's no way... He was dying in that
opening..."

"Opening?" Crusch aims a questioning gaze at the two adults. "Is that
what it's called? How'd you come by such information?"

Though she didn't mean to sound too demanding, the duchess wasn't the
only one interested, as Anastasia piped from the other side of the same
row to speak with the parents on her own.

"Couldn't hurt t' help us get knowledge about these things,
Natsuki-kun's folks." She said with a bit of excitement in her eyes.
"You two are from a different world than us Lugunican bunch... And we
want what's best for yer son... So?"

"What's best for my son?" Kenichi looked at the merchant queen with eyes
full of hesitation, which made Anastasia lean back with an eyebrow
raised.

The parents both seemed to be contemplating what they'd watched, which
led them to be closed off from the rest of the cast. Something Anastasia
didn't anticipate, and for that... For that... She seemed to be eager to
ignore Subaru's multiple deaths in such a small moment in favor of
conversing and socializing with other people in the theater.

"... fufufu~" Priscilla's pleased chuckles filled the air at the
misfortune of Anastasia, enjoying her misshape to a far greater degree.

"I-I'm not sure what any of this is, to be honest... I..." Kenichi found
himself muttering without confidence anymore. He looked to his wife some
more and found himself seeking some help. "I can't believe... It looked
like the world was showing an intro to his 'adventure'... No? Naoko?"

The mother herself bit her lip and looked at their son. "It seemed that
way..."

"He used to watch a lot of these Isekai stories back home before...
Before he was taken..." Kenichi muttered some more into his hand as he
moved it toward his chin.

"And he would read it too... That's what he told me once when he took
too long at the convenience store." Noako held a small frown. "I would
ask him not to spend time browsing and reading, or else he'd give the
wrong idea to the seller and get them excited that he was going to buy
something from them."

"Naoko..." Kenichi held on to his wife, giving her a look of sympathy as
she trembled slightly at the memory. "It's okay... He is here right
now."

"But we can't even talk to him!" Naoko sniffled while holding her son's
other hand, the one not occupied by Emilia. "You saw how he was running
through every death... You saw him... He was running and running as if
the world didn't matter anymore! His deaths..." Naoko's face had the
tears flowing shortly after as she kept holding on to her son. "How can
he just run through his bodies like this?! Subaru! Please answer me...
Please..."

Kenichi held on to his wife with a look of helplessness. Unable to say
anything. Unable to do anything. Just a worthless man watching his
family collapse.

Around them, there were eyes that couldn't continue watching the couple
due to sheer emotion. Some, like Crusch and Rem, looked away out of
respect.

"... Isekai stories..." Beatrice muttered, sitting back to reach
Subaru's chest with the back of her head. Her eyes kept staring at the
floor, thinking about the fact that she saw her mother on the screen and
how much hurt Subaru will be going through. "Foreshadowing such a thing
as you dying again... Subaru..." She whispered while trying to reach for
either of his arms, but the sleeping boy had two ladies on his sides
holding on to him.

Leaving Beatrice alone to just sit on his lap with her eyes empty.
Brutally so.

"No matter how many times you die... huh..." Emilia sighed from the
side, her eyes still glistening and staring at her boots. "You really
are a dummy... You dunderhead... You lair..."

"Does it change your mindset now, Emilia-sama?" Rem asked from behind
the half-elf and her knight, looking at her with pain clear in her eyes.

Emilia's gaze was surprised as she turned to Rem. "My mindset?"

"The one where you said you'd be accepting of what he's doing now."
Rem's words were striking as Emilia's eyes flashed with the memories of
Subaru's run through his corpses in the opening.

"Do you still agree to that way of doing things? To support this
behavior from him?" Rem asked with her voice lower than usual, not
wanting anyone else to be privy to what she was saying to the half-elf.
Still... the maid had a tone of pleading in her voice.

One that wasn't lost on Emilia as the half-elf looked at the sleeve of
Subaru's jacket with pursed lips.

It was only a moment, but Rem realized that Emilia didn't respond to her
pleas for common sense because Emilia didn't have any refutation to
give. The girl was merely contemplating as if she was a maiden presented
with multiple strange cakes on her name day.

Rem couldn't help but grit her teeth as she remembered the same memories
as Emilia did. How could she not? They had just watched Subaru's
sacrificial nature in such a clear way that neither of them could deny
it.

"Subaru must reeeeally be lonely... To feel like he can die over and
over again for us..." Emilia's soft mumbles found their way to Rem's
ears. And for a moment the maid held on to her breath.

"Yes." Rem sighed. Letting go of her skirt and ruffling it back to shape
as her nerves gave out. "Yes... Subaru-kun feels lonely..."

"I won't let him be lonely after he wakes up... I promise..." Emilia's
confident words were said without her trying to make eye contact with
Rem.

And they didn't need to look at each other to agree on this one point.
Whether Rem doesn't agree with Emilia or not.

Both girls could at least feel the same determination even through their
different view points.

"I won't accept that he's hurting himself for me---for us. But... I
agree that I won't let him have the chance to sacrifice himself either."
Rem affirmed, taking to look at Subaru's head while crossing her legs as
she leans back in her seat.

There didn't need to be more said between them. Whatever argument that
Rem wanted to make had died when Emilia couldn't give a reaction to
remembering Subaru's butchering other than glancing away.

"Crusch-sama?" Felix asked from the side by the time Emilia and Rem were
talking to each other, earning him a small glare as the duchess had been
trying to eavesdrop. The knight didn't flinch, however, as he began to
speak unfettered. "Do you think you'll be alright to watch what happens
next?"

At this, Crusch paused. A deep one that showed surprise on her usually
reserved expression.

"What's the meaning of that statement, Ferris?" Crusch raised her
eyebrow. "Have you become afraid of what comes next?" Though she sounded
like she was teasing, Crusch wasn't the type to ask such a question
lightly, as she gave her full sincerity behind the care she showed her
knight.

"Ferri-chan would never feel scared when he's beside his lady, nya!"
Felix couldn't help but let out a yelp of happiness with the way she
overprotected his meager feelings. "Jeez... You really are remarkable,
Crusch-sama."

"What...? I apologize, but I don't find anything I have done sufficient
for such a compliment yet." Crusch sighed and turned her head away from
Felix to glance toward Subaru.

Felix couldn't help but feel his heart ache slightly with the way she
was focusing on the black-haired knight at the front.

He sighed and continued his question. "I was wondering when... When he
left our protection after the White Whale was thwarted... Isn't it over
for our role in the story, so to say?"

Crusch looked at her knight, her eyes showing thoughtfulness as she read
between the lines expertly.

"You mean to say that we have no more part to play in Natsuki Subaru's
story? I thought that would've been clear since what we are watching---"

"Why are we still watching, then?" Felix blurted out.

"... Are you suggesting that we might leave the viewings to the rest of
the people here just because we aren't a party concerned by the coming
events?" Crusch held him with a sudden firmness in her eyes.

"... I know that we care about him, Crusch-sama." Felix steadily walked
this road with his face nervous yet brave. "But..." He let the moment
pass so he could be sure to continue saying his piece. Oddly enough,
Crusch was allowing him to do it with a patience he never expected.
"But... What's the plan here? Do we just continue watching this
depressing story even when it doesn't concern us anymore, nya?"

"Ferris, you disappoint me greatly." Crusch sounded baffled by her
knight's audacity. "Has he not given up the most to help us achieve
great peace across our world? Do we not owe him, as his allies and
friends, to watch any more suffering he goes through?"

"But what if that ends up hurting us more than we can handle,
Crusch-sama?" Felix responds with a bite in his lip, but Crusch would
only glare at him.

"Speak clearly... What is it that you mean by this?" She demands of her
knight.

Felix held her gaze with his own anxious one... His own protective
one... His own scared one...

"After we watch what comes next... You'll be thrown by the wayside
again, Crusch-sama..."

Her eyes widened due to his brutal honesty as he all but leaned forward
to emphasize his words to her. "... Nya... I can't handle seeing you
break or be forgotten yet again... And knowing him... He will be too
busy fighting his damn hardest protecting those around him."

"Ferris" Crusch's tone silenced her trembling knight as she moved her
gloved hand to weave her fingers through his hair. "You think far too
much about the most ridiculous of things."

"..." He let her pat his head and sighed. Looking at the ground
obediently.

"I refuse to be forgotten behind anyone in this room. Even if Natsuki
Subaru would go on without me." Crusch grinned down at her knight with
confidence that surprised him. "Do not fret, Ferris. I haven't come to
be so pathetic that I'd be alright with such an ending for us."

"Us?" Felix looked at her in surprise. "Crusch-sama?"

"Of course." She held his head in the palm of her hand with care. Giving
him a grin. "Who's going to take you with them in this room other than
me? Priscilla?"

Felix shuddered at the memory of the last time he was alone with the red
princess, leaning further into Crusch's palm and shuffling his head
against it.

"Don't remind me of that wench, nya!"

Crusch gives in to her chuckles and ruffles his hair further. "Then we
are stuck with one another, as always."

Felix's smile didn't reach his eyes as he looked up to his lady. "Yes,
Crusch-sama... As always..."

"You are a cruel woman, Crusch-sama," Wilhelm murmured, his voice so low
it barely carried over the soft sobbing of the Natsuki parents a few
rows away.

From the seat directly beside them from Felix's side, Wilhelm watched
the exchange with the practiced stillness of a man who had lived long
enough to see the same tragedy play out in different costumes.

He saw the way Crusch's fingers moved through Felix's hair. To an
outsider, it was a gesture of deep intimacy and comfort. To Wilhelm, it
looked like a mercy killing.

"It is a heavy thing, is it not?"

Wilhelm glanced to his other side. Frederica was watching him, her
demi-human eyes sharp and knowing. Beside her, little Petra sat like a
statue, her small hands balled into fists so tight her skin looked like
parchment.

The girl hadn't spoken since the 'opening' ended. She seemed to be
shielding herself from the theater's atmosphere by simply refusing to
participate in it.

"Devotion, Frederica-dono?" Wilhelm asked.

"The realization that devotion can become a cage," Frederica replied
softly, her gaze flickering toward Rem, then back to Felix. "Crusch-sama
knows that if she doesn't push him away now, he will simply wither in
the shadow of whatever she becomes after this. She is being 'cruel' to
give him a chance to survive without her."

"A bitter medicine," Wilhelm noted. "One I have seen many fail to
swallow."

Wilhelm looked at Crusch some more. "She really is cruel."

He didn't mean it as an insult. In fact, there was a tinge of respect in
his tired eyes. He saw exactly what she was doing. "Forcing Felix-dono
to choose between sticking with his obsession with a woman who's heart
is already set on Natsuki Subaru... Or choose to move on from his one
true lady."

"Subaru-sama doesn't make it easy," Frederica added with a faint, sad
smile. "He has a way of making everyone feel like they are the center of
his world, even when he is dying for a hundred others at the same time.
It makes people... possessive. Even the kind ones."

Petra didn't contribute. She just stared at the back of Subaru's head,
her silence more deafening than any comment could have been.

Suddenly, a sharp snort erupted from the row in front of them. Felt was
slumped in her seat, her boots kicked up on the armrest beside her. She
didn't turn around, but her voice was loud enough to cut through the
heavy philosophizing, yet low enough to be ignored by Felix and Crusch.

"Spirits, you people are depressing," Felt barked, her eyes still tired
but defiant at the same time. "Is there a single person in this room who
isn't busy tying themselves into knots over who loves who? Big Bro's out
here getting shot down while running through a fuckin' tunnel of death
and stabbed by psychos on a repeat, and you're back here analyzing the
'cruelty' of a headpat."

"Felt-sama, please," Reinhard began, his voice habitual and weary, but
she cut him off with a sharp wave of her hand.

"No, 'please' nothing!" Felt finally turned her head, her red eyes
locking onto Wilhelm with a fierce intensity. "Hey, Old Man. If you've
got so much spare time to worry about Catboy and his lady problems, why
don't you use those 'wise eyes' of yours to look about three feet to the
left?"

Wilhelm stiffened. He knew exactly what, and who, she was referring to.

Felt gestured crudely toward Reinhard, who sat rigid, his gaze fixed
forward, his expression a mask of perfect, agonizing composure.

"Talk to your grandson, Old Guy," Felt said, her tone losing its bite
and becoming something closer to a command. "Before the next 'vision'
starts and we all have to watch Big Bro die again. Ye're over here
worried about some sad loser being 'forgotten,' while the guy who
actually shares your blood is sitting right there, looking like he's
already a ghost. Sort your own mess out."

Wilhelm opened his mouth to respond, to offer a dismissal, a platitude
about the *'Astrea way'*, but the words died in his throat. He looked at
Reinhard's back. The Sword Saint looked invincible, a pillar of
strength. But from this angle, Wilhelm could see the slight tremor in
his grandson's hands.

Encouraged by Felt's bluntness, or perhaps simply pushed to the edge by
the weight of the White Whale's viewing being so long ago, Reinhard
finally turned. He didn't look like the Kingdom's strongest asset, he
looked like a boy trying to navigate a minefield.

"Grandfather," Reinhard began, his voice surprisingly steady despite the
tension. "I... I wish to ask you something. Regarding the previous
viewing."

Wilhelm didn't turn his head, but his eyes shifted toward the youth.
"Speak."

"Your reaction to the elf... to Sphinx," Reinhard said, his blue eyes
searching Wilhelm's hardened face. "I have never seen you lose your
composure in such a way. Not even during the fight with the Whale. Why
did her presence provoke such... such absolute hatred?"

The air around Wilhelm seemed to drop ten degrees. He finally turned his
body to face Reinhard, his hands resting heavily on his knees.

"Sphinx was a monster of the Demi-human War, Reinhard-dono," Wilhelm
said, his voice like grinding stones. "A biological filth that should
have remained buried. She was the reason for more suffering than you can
comprehend. And..." He paused, his gaze darkening. "She was a great
terror to the woman who's meant to be the grandmother of that man."

He jerked his chin toward Roswaal, who sat further away, his expression
unreadable behind his makeup.

"I demand answers," Wilhelm growled. "I want to know why Roswaal is
housing a creature that bears the same face as that monster who'd
slaughtered so many."

"Then why aren't you attacking him?" Felt's voice cut in again, sharp
and impatient. She was leaning over the back of her seat now. "If you're
so pissed off, Old Man, go over there and squeeze his neck until he
talks. Why are we just sitting here?"

"Because he is protected," a new voice said.

It was Petra. The young girl didn't look up, her voice sounding hollow
and devoid of its usual childish warmth. She talked, with her gaze fixed
on the blank screen.

"When we were told about Warden-sama... Whoever took his place in charge
of this place," Petra continued, her tone chillingly flat. "It protected
Roswaal-sama when Garfiel-san tried to attack him earlier. It doesn't
matter how much we hate him or what he's hiding. It's unfair..."

Felt snorted, but she didn't disagree. Instead, she turned her sights on
Frederica. "And what about you? You're the one who gave Big Bro that
letter, right? The one that sent him to this 'Sanctuary' place in the
first place. Roswaal's got a plan, and you're the one who opened the
door for him."

Frederica's expression didn't flicker. She stood straight, her hands
clasped in front of her in a perfect maid's stance. "I performed my
duties as instructed, Felt-sama. I have no information to give regarding
my master's private affairs."

"Doubt that," Felt muttered dryly. "You're all the same. Secrets on top
of secrets."

"There is more at play," Petra whispered, her voice almost ghostly.
"Roswaal-sama isn't just being protected because he's a participant.
He's being used too..."

"Petra, that is enough," Frederica said softly, shushing the girl with a
firm but gentle hand on her shoulder.

Wilhelm looked back at Reinhard. He saw the conflict in his grandson's
eyes, a desire to protect the fragile peace of the theater or seek
justice and answers.

"The child is right, Reinhard," Wilhelm said, giving a grim nod toward
Petra. "It is clear that none of us can get a clear answer while the
entity holding this theater is actively shielding him. We are not
audience members... we are prisoners of a story that hasn't finished
being told."

Reinhard lowered his head, the weight of his own powerlessness in this
room finally sinking in. "I understand, Grandfather."

"That fuckin' sucks." Felt huffed, eyeing Petra some more, but rolling
her eyes to look somewhere else.

**CLICK**

The air in the theater suddenly vibrated with a low, bone-deep hum. The
screen, which had been a dull, obsidian void, began to pulse with a
faint light with a flow of imagery that kept playing at such a speed
that no one could tell what each image was portraying.

The conversations that had been splintering into small, bitter groups
died instantly as every head turned toward the display.

"Wait! We can't just... we shouldn't just start the next one yet!" Otto
stood up, his hands waving frantically. His face was pale, his eyes
darting between the screen and his own camp. "We haven't even processed
what we just saw! There are too many things left unsaid, and if we keep
watching without a plan, we're just going to---"

"What we '/should/' do is get the truth," Julius interrupted, his voice
cutting through Otto's panic like a rapier.

He stood as well, his posture perfect, but his hand was white-knuckled
where it rested on his hip, not that anyone could see beneath his glove.
"You speak of discussion, yet your camp remains a fortress of silence. I
demand to know about this Witch of Greed and that person named Sphinx.
If Roswaal L. Mathers is housing an entity that shares the face of a
monster from the Great War, then this is no longer a private matter of
the Emilia Camp. It is a threat to the kingdom."

Julius turned his gaze to Otto, his expression softening only slightly.
"Otto. You are a man of reason. Trust me. Tell me what you and Garfiel
are hiding. We are your allies, aren't we?"

"It's not that simple, Julius-san!" Otto cried out. "Garfiel's problem
wasn't even about any witch present within the Sanctuary! It was---"

"It was about everythin'!" Garfiel snarled, almost leaping out of his
chair. He didn't look at Julius, simply turned around to look at Roswaal
in the back with a feral, murderous intensity. "I ain't gonna be askin'
for pity or understandin' out 'ere! I blame that makeup-wearin' bastard
for everythin' that's happenin' to Cap'n! He's the one who let all this
rot happen!"

"And that's precisely what we need to understand!" Tivey piped up from
the Anastasia camp, his monocle glinting in the screen's light. "The
Emilia camp is hoarding information like dragons. You watched the intro,
didn't you? We saw Natsuki Subaru die a dozen times in seconds before
this even started. His safety is clearly at zero, and you're all
standing around him like a wall of secrets. How can we help him if we
don't know what he's fighting?"

The parents watched the chaos from their seats. Naoko didn't look at the
arguing knights. Her eyes were fixed on Emilia, who looked smaller than
ever, and then shifted to Rem.

The blue-haired maid looked utterly lost. Having been in a coma during
the events being shown, she was witnessing the disintegration of her
friends' trust in real-time.

Kenichi, however, felt a gaze on the back of his neck. He turned
slightly to see Priscilla sitting in the very last row. She was lounging
like a queen on a throne of boredom, a fan partially obscuring a sharp,
knowing grin.

She caught his eye and gave a slow, deliberate wink.

The message was clear.

/Look at them. See how they squabble while your son bleeds. My offer
remains. Bring him to me, and he will be a king, not a martyr./

Kenichi's jaw tightened, but he looked away, his eyes landing on Crusch.
Unlike the others, she was still. She sat with her chin resting on her
hand, her eyes contemplative as she weighed the room's energy.

"Enough," Crusch said. It wasn't a shout, but the authority in her voice
acted like a physical weight, forcing the room into a sudden, shaky
silence.

She looked at Julius. "Julius Juukilius, stand down. Your pursuit of
justice is noble, but your timing is flawed. If we wish for answers,
they are literally inches away from manifesting on that screen. Why
argue over fragments when the whole truth is about to be forced upon
us?"

Julius hesitated, his eyes flickering toward his lady.

"Oh? My, Crusch-san~, you certainly sound authoritative today,"
Anastasia remarked, her tone playful but her eyes narrowing into cold
slits. "But I don't recall givin' ya the right to command my Knight. Ya
might wanna remember whose camp ye're talkin' to before ya start issuin'
orders."

Crusch didn't even blink at the thinly veiled threat. She simply gave a
dry ghost of a smile.

"I merely wanted to see if you were still alive over there,
Anastasia-sama. You've been quiet for a minute too long while your
knight disrupted my affairs, I worried the theater had claimed you."

Anastasia snorted, a sharp, un-ladylike sound. She waved a hand
dismissively toward Julius. "Back down, Julius. She's right, even if
she's bein' a bit of a nag about it. Let's see what the big show has for
us next."

Julius bowed stiffly and took his seat, the tension in his shoulders not
leaving for a second. Otto sank back into his chair, wiping sweat from
his brow, while Garfiel continued to vibrate with suppressed rage.

"That was so funny," Meili giggled, her feet dangling off the edge of
her seat. She looked at the screen, her eyes shimmering with a dark,
childish glee. "I wonder if Onii-san can hear how much of a fuss people
make over him while he sleeps."

She turned her head to Schult. "What do you think, Rosie-chan? Don't you
think that they're annoying someone who's very scary~?"

Schult, sitting directly beside her, didn't giggle. He glared at the
assassin girl, his small face contorted with a mixture of anger and a
strange kind of pity. "You should be quiet, Meili-san. You shouldn't say
such mean things when everyone is hurting."

Meili turned her head, her smile sharpening. "Oh? And why not? Onii-san
looks so much more interesting when he's not acting like some hero,
doesn't he? To keep going even after seeing a pile of your own
corpses... it's almost like a game!"

"Liar," Schult snapped.

The sudden edge in his voice caused Meili's smile to falter for a split
second. Schult leaned in closer, his voice low enough that only she
could hear. "I was right here. When the music was playing and he was
falling... I felt you shaking. You weren't laughing then... You were
afraid of him."

Meili's expression hardened into a mask of mockery. She reached out and
flicked Schult's forehead with a sharp clack. "You talk too much for a
little puppy. Maybe I should sew your mouth shut so you can't tell any
more stories."

Schult didn't flinch. He rubbed his forehead but kept his eyes locked on
hers. "You can be as mean as you want. But if you keep hiding behind
these lies, you're going to end up alone. You won't have any friends,
Meili-san."

Meili opened her mouth to deliver a scathing retort, but her gaze
inadvertently drifted down to the row below them.

There sat Petra, hunched over, her silence a stark contrast to Meili's
loud cruelty. Meili's purple eyes lingered on the maid-in-training for a
long moment, a flash of something unreadable, envy, or perhaps
curiosity, crossing her face.

"Friends are for dummies," Meili whispered, though the bite was gone
from her tone.

In the very back, away from the prying eyes of the candidates, Ram stood
behind Roswaal. The Margrave's makeup was as immaculate as ever, but in
the dim, pulsing light of the screen, he looked like a marionette with
its strings tangled.

"Master," Ram said, her voice a calm anchor in the theater's chaotic
sea. "What are you thinking?"

Roswaal tilted his head back, giving her a playful, exaggerated wink
that didn't reach his mismatched eyes. "Thinking? Why, Ram, my d~eee~ar,
I'm thinking that the lighting in this room is dreadfully unflattering
to my complexion!"

"Stop it," Ram commanded. Her voice didn't rise, but it held a finality
that silenced his theatrical hum. "What have you prepared for when the
'Warden' shows the rest? When every secret you've buried in that
Sanctuary is brought to light? Do you have a plan?"

Roswaal looked at her. For a heartbeat, the mask slipped.

Ram didn't see a mastermind.

She saw a man who was utterly, completely helpless. He was a scholar who
had lost his book, a wizard who had run out of spells. He had prepared
for a world that followed a script, and the script had been burned
before his eyes.

Not only that, but he gave a small, genuine shrug, his shoulders
drooping.

Ram let out a long, weary sigh. She reached up and adjusted the collar
of his coat, her touch lingering for a second longer than necessary.

"You really are a hopeless fool of a master," she murmured, her insult
wrapped in a layer of fierce, unwavering devotion. "I suppose I'll just
have to keep picking up your pieces, even when the world sees how broken
you truly are."

Roswaal didn't respond. He simply looked up at the screen as the hum
reached a crescendo.

The viewing had begun.

*Season 2 Episode 3*

*Begin.*

*Loud bells start to ring across the green field of grass, as well as
waves of wind that could only be indicated by the loud rustling of the
verdant blades.*

"Starts right where it stopped, huh..." Heinkel muttered from his seat,
reaching for his pocket but stopping himself when Priscilla moved to fan
herself.

"Shitty fuckin' castle of dreams." A voice muttered next to Priscilla,
which gained the attention of Heinkel immediately.

"Castle?" Heinkel raised an eyebrow towards Al. "Why'd you say that?
Where the fuck is a castle to be in a large plain field like that?"

"..." Al didn't respond to Heinkel with anything noteworthy.

This led to Heinkel turning away from Al and then looking to Priscilla,
who was sitting between them with her eyes closely watching the screen
and nothing else. As if they were not worthy enough for her attention,
she would zone them out.

"Damn... Ain't ya fuckin' rude." Heinkel snorts while turning away from
the two completely, settling himself back to get comfortable for the new
viewing. "Fuckin' weirdo." He couldn't help but be petty one more time
for being ignored by the helmeted knight next to Priscilla.

"*My name is Echidna..."*

*The lady, underneath an umbrella in the said grass field, spoke, her
voice smooth and feminine with an increasingly appealing tilt.*

*Subaru faced her from the bottom of his hill. She gave him a small
smile.*

"*Or perhaps you would know me better as the Witch of Greed?"*

"Honey..." Naoko held on to her husband as they watched the beginning of
a new viewing with nothing to be sure of about the people around them.

"..." Kenichi's eyes would harden as soon as he noticed the woman on the
screen. "I know, honey... My heart is screaming at me to be careful the
moment I see that smile."

"Look at Subaru..." Naoko said while holding on to her husband. "How
come he doesn't trust her either?"

"What are you..." Kenichi didn't understand why his wife would ask him
such a question...

"Can't you see... He's always been too kind for his own good... Yet now
he starts by being nervous and distrustful of anyone he meets for the
first time..." Naoko's voice trembled, her eyes never leaving the boy on
the screen.

At that, Kenichi's breath hitched. He was so focused on the danger that
could be presented to his son that he forgot to see the change in his
own boy.

He could see that his son was no longer trying to waste his body on
useless movements to be cool or cautious. Subaru's eyes were scanning
for exits even when he was in an open field with someone as mysterious
as this Echidna.

The over-the-top energy Kenichi had spent years trying to instill in him
had been replaced by a cold, sharpened edge.

"He's just being cautious..." Kenichi would whisper to his wife, hollow,
without conviction.

The two held their hands together with Naoko putting more into her touch
on Subaru's hand.

Within the room, there were two sets of different reactions to Echidna's
name.

The first and most obvious ones were the ones who heard the name for the
very first time.

"And so it begins..." Julius muttered with his eyes holding little
contempt for the woman on the screen already. "A Witch who's alive
during our lifetime... Someone naming herself with the name Greed at
that..."

"Would you stop glancing toward us while saying that with such a tone?"
Otto couldn't hold himself back from showing his grimace, feeling the
hardened eyes of betrayal from the knight behind him.

"I have no idea what you mean, but perhaps a guilty conscience can make
you feel all sorts of things, hm?" Julius looked down at the merchant
with as much coldness as Emilia's icicles.

Otto shook his head with exasperation, looking at Julius with pleading
intent. "We are as much in the dark as you guys are. This woman... It's
my first time seeing her."

"So? You still know how this all ends, regardless of whether he Returns
by Death or not. Yet you refuse to tell us anything." Julius countered
Otto's heartfelt plea with cold logic.

Otto looked defeated before they even started, seeming to accept Julius'
logic.

"We... We don't even know if this is the true loop because the meetings
between him and this Witch weren't something he confided in us with..."
Otto's eyes showed their own betrayal but not from Julius.

"Oi, Bright Knight..." Garfiel gained both of their attention before
Julius could say anything to that. "Can ya fuck off? Pleasantly' of
course."

Otto's face contorted in shock at the threatening glints Garfiel was
giving Julius.

"What are you doing?! That's no way to speak to our friend!" Otto
demanded an explanation, looking between the two men with panic.

Julius himself seemed to hide his hurt well, if he felt any, as he
stared Garfiel down.

"So you choose to be rude to someone who wishes to be your friend,
Garfiel?"

"Ain't no friend of mine amazin' selfs life gonna be someone who killed
Cap'n... So fuck off." Garfiel's cold words were spoken so stiffly and
emotionlessly that they delayed the reactions appropriate to them.

Before Otto could smack Garfiel, though his fist was delayed from the
sheer whiplash of how quickly this had escalated, Julius reacted first.

The knight merely gave a humble nod and flashed a waving hand towards
Otto, canceling any violence from the merchant.

Otto was left to openly stare at Garfiel as Julius was driven away from
them further than the trio had ever been since the start of the
viewings.

"Why?!" He demanded of the blonde, who strangely enough kept a cold
facade going as he looked up at the screen. "Why did you say that?!"

Garfiel didn't respond, even when Otto shook him though the merchant had
no strength to actually move Garfiel's body when the blonde tenses his
muscles. So Otto gave up on the idea of demanding physically, merely
settling back in his seat.

Among the wave of the people still reacting, another person who was
included within the boy's group but far away from them to be of any
assistance to the increasing fracture of their friendship, Reinhard was
observing the screen with increasing curiosity, though he more so
observed the others's reactions rather than give his own.

"Interesting..." Crusch muttered into her gloved hand, crossing her legs
as she focused her gaze from the screen to her side, to the person
sitting next to Rem. "Why would you be that excited about this woman
specifically, Anastasia-sama?"

"Ahhh~... Echidna..." Anastasia herself showed a wide grin that
contained a lot of excitement. "It ain't gonna be of use to lie to ya
with that divine protection, Crusch-san~... But this ain't boutta be my
confession scene. Sorry."

"I'd not force you to answer me... I was merely curious." Crusch's
features softened, shaking her head as she turned to the screen.

She tried to ignore just how /giddy/ Anastasia's suddenly became.

Crusch's eyes landed slightly on the blonde drills that could be noticed
on Subaru due to the spirit using him as her seat.

Now we move to the people who know exactly who this was. The hidden gems
of the theater who'd already been acquainted with Echidna and were lucky
enough to be bestowed with the holy connection of the witch before the
rest.

The ones who should be comfortable seeing her on screen with Subaru.

*Subaru glared at her with an increasingly anxious look, which only made
her smile widen more in a mocking, maidenly expression of innocence.*

"*Please, it hurts to see you look so alarmed by me." She opens her eyes
to give him an innocent pout. "Despite who I am, I'm still a tender
young maiden, you know."*

*He seemed to look at the lady atop the hill with more resolve. "Sorry,
but..." He gives a small, shaky smile. "There's no way I'm* /*not*/
*going to be alarmed when you tell me you're the Witch of Greed the
moment we meet."*

"*I see. That's a fair point." She gives him a slight smirk before
giggling with delight.*

*A giggle that rang out through the field of grass as if nothing else
existed with her voice.*

"What is she playing at?" Beatrice muttered to no one in particular.
"Four hundred years... And such an imitation can't even muster the true
form of Mother's smile, in fact. Such a faulty creation."\\
"I didn't know Subaru had met with her before I did..." Emilia from
beside the spirit was having an entirely different outlook. While
Beatrice was more curious and begrudging toward Echidna, Emilia was far
more cautious and distrusting.

"I hope he will be able to handle how nasty and mean she can be..."
Emilia whispered softly, stroking Subaru's hand beside her as she
remembered her own meeting with Echidna.

"Calling herself a young maiden while acting like some comic relief for
a silly man before her... How utterly wretched." Roswaal all but seethed
beside Ram in the back as he noted every single aspect of the woman with
nothing but disdain.

In the same row as him, someone was having the opposite reaction.

"This has to be a joke... Don't tell me I actually feel excited to see
her again with all her goofy acts trying to be human... Teacher..." Al
sighed in his helmet, leaning down to stare at Echidna even more clearly
through the slits.

"*What is this place?" Subaru asked from the bottom of the hill. "I was
in some dark ruins just a moment ago! When did you move me?!"*

"*Unfortunately, you're mistaken. You weren't physically moved
anywhere." Echidna answers breezily from her chair beneath the umbrella.
"You were merely invited to my tea party."*

"*Tea party?" Subaru had a curious expression on his face now.*

*Echidna closes her eyes and explains with a smile. "A witch's tea
party."*

"She seems nice," Schult commented anxiously, looking toward his lady
when he heard her snort.

"Have more perception, Schult. That woman is no more deserving of our
time than the dirt beneath mine divine heel."

"Priscilla-sama?" Schult tilted his head as Priscilla snorted, giving
the witch on the screen a mocking grin.

"Tis nothing to concern your cute head about," She waved at him,
cancelling his attention from her. "This divine merely found amusement
at such a folly being played on the commoners of this world."

"Whatcha mean by that?" Heinkel asked as politely as he could, showing
as much intrigue as Schult and Meili now as they heard what Priscilla
had to say.

The confidence she gave as she judged Echidna before anything could be
explained about the Witch was a spectacle to the two children and
Heinkel.

But it was much more than that to the knight who'd turned his head to
look at Priscilla with widened eyes.

"Don't you see how far she goes to act human? She's far more dependent
on our world than we are led to believe... That /Witch/ isn't worth your
consideration for being nice or anything more, Schult."

"I see..." Schult shrugged and looked back at the screen, feeling safe
to be there beside someone as confident as his lady.

"Something the matter, Aldebaran?" Priscilla would ask, glancing with
her head tilted at the man next to her.

Al's quiet was almost broken then and there, as his helmet stared at the
lady with something ominous and foreboding. An omen of sheer negativity
as she grinned at him with her full confidence on display with her red
irises shining in their beauty.

"Nothing, Princess." Al turned away from her with a hint of gratitude
that couldn't be explained to those unaware of Al. Which seemed to be
everyone in the theater as he was one of the most offish and calm
characters inside the room.

Except for one more person other than Priscilla who truly does notice Al
from time to time.

Ram looked away from the helmeted knight after seeing Priscilla wasn't
giving him any trouble.

*The screen zooms out to highlight the fact that Subaru was still at the
bottom of the grassy hill while she sat in front of her tea set on top.*

"*Are you going to stand there forever?" She opens her arm for him to
invite himself closer. "Why don't you at least sit down before the tea
gets cold?"*

*Subaru seemed to hesitate for a second before he shook his head
roughly. "All right, fine!" He stomped up the hill to reach the lady.*

"Emilia-sama...?" Petra sounded from the back, pausing the screen and
bringing attention on herself as well as the half-elf by everyone in the
theater.

This was the first time Petra was showing an emotion which was
determination or curiosity since the last viewing had ended.

"Yes, Petra?" Emilia gazed back at the little maid with soft eyes.
"What's the matter?"

"... Did you meet her as well?" Petra gestured to Echidna on the screen.
Finding eyes widening among the room.

"Why would Emilia-sama have met with the Witch?" Tivey asked with alarm.

Emilia seemed to have an easy time ignoring the attention on her after
having been subject to this scenario far too many times. She merely held
Ptra with her gaze and gave a small nod.

"I've met Echidna as well, yes... In my trials, though... She never
offered me any tea or was this nice to me." Emilia muttered with little
disappointment as she trailed back to the screen.

While everyone widened their eyes at the revelation from Emilia, gaining
her curious looks from Crusch and the parents, Petra was the one to ask
the next question.

"Is she... safe?"

"Safe?" Emilia tilted her head for an elaboration.

"Is she a bad guy...?"

Schult would straighten his back behind Petra's seat. Since he was the
on closest in the back row, he was more invested due to what his lady
was saying about Echidna a moment ago.

Emilia had the entire room other than Roswaal, Beatrice, and Al looking
at her with baited breath.

"It's not... She can be mean? She's a lot more dishonest about her
feelings than I'd like to consider healthy... But." Emilia trailed off
for a moment, noticing that Beatrice was giving her a raised brow.

"But?" Naoko urged from the side, beside Emilia. "Speak up, honey.
Please."

"But... She's got a good heart." Emilia's statement founded a sense of
relief that ran across the room. Though another wave of disdain ran
alongside the first one from select people.

"Yeah right... Bet anyone who's not a failed project of hers gets that
feeling about her too." Al muttered softly, glaring away from Emilia's
stupidity.

While Roswaal glared at her openly with disgust and distaste, clicking
his teeth.

Beatrice meanwhile, had the most tame reaction among the Echidna squad
of the cast. As a matter of fact...

The small spirit had a smile at Emilia's sincere words...

*Before a second had passed from when he reached the top, he sat down
and gulped a teacup that was already reserved for him by the Witch.*

*Said witch was looking at him with a blinking expression. As if
befuddled by his behavior, he set the cup down with a loud 'clank.'*

Al choked as soon as he saw her expression on the screen.

"... How could you not thank her for the honors, in fact?!" Beatrice
could only shriek after blinking in complete astonishment as Ricardo and
Felt let loose a laughter so loud they could immediately be heard
falling from their seats.

"Is he a fool?" Crusch wondered to herself, looking at the floor for a
second before shaking her head at Felix who actually was trying to
answer that question. "Never mind." She palmed her face while Frederica
held her hand to her mouth out of surprise.

Emilia and Rem both sighed at the same time, looking very much tired at
the antics displayed by Subaru.

"You could ask what it contains before drinking? Maybe?!" Rem mumbled
with a frustrated pout.

"Dunderhead..." Emilia whined into her hand as she hid her face in both
palms.

"Onii-san's going to die from that isn't he?" Meili surprised Schult by
sounding almost somber when saying that.

"We hope not..." Schult commented, giving her a small smile, thinking
she was worried for Subaru.

"... That would be so lame! What a loser way to die!" Meili's expression
of outrage and disappointment didn't hint that she was seeing the flaws
in her logic. And so Schult merely turned away from her with a
deadpanned face.

Garfiel was letting out a small scoff with a look of admiration on his
face. "Cap'n's got them in metal and size..." Though the blonde didn't
have the usual cheer he'd give for Subaru's idiotic tendencies.

Beside him, Otto just held on to his head from both sides, covering his
ears and looking at the ceiling like a drowned patient with no soul in
his stare which aimed at God himself. It was all he could do to convulse
all his energy so he could stop himself from jumping and sending Subaru
a fresh knuckle sandwich.

Behind them, though more quiet than he'd like to be, Julius felt his
entire body collapse as he leaned back with the same stare of death
aiming for a god in the ceiling of the room so he can pray to them and
end his suffering.

"He just drank that...?" Anastasia was still blinking with amazement.
"The stupid displayed right now must make ya doubt yer education of the
boy, huh?" She couldn't help but tease the parents a bit, trying to add
levity in the worst way possible.

Kenichi and Noako looked at Anastasia together with innocent
expressions. "What are you talking about? Whose education?"

Anastasia blinked again but this time at the parents who stared back at
her like befuddled cats. "The education of your son." She pointed at
Subaru with her head, glaring at Kenichi a bit.

"I have no son." Kenichi's innocent expression didn't change while Naoko
seemed not to understand anything but she trusted her husband so she
imitated him like a cat.

Anastasia realized that Subaru was just disowned for being so stupid and
she resulted to look up at the ceiling with her own stare towards God.

"*You're awfully brave, gulping down tea served by a witch in one
gulp."*

"*Hah! If you really wanted to kill me, I'd be a pile of ash the very
next instant." Subaru smirked, though a bead of sweat could be seen
running down his cheek.*

"*What's this tea, anyway? It's not bad, but it's not good, either."
Subaru looked down at his cup curiously.*

"*It's something that's produced here." Echidna began to explain, "To
put it bluntly, it's a body fluid of mine."*

"*WHAT THE HELL DID YOU JUST MAKE ME DRINK?!" Subaru shouted, standing
up and slamming his hand on the table multiple times.*

It was Petra's turn to look away to the far off wall and stare
deadpanned for a god somewhere as the disgust overtook the room.

"EW!" Mimi blew her tongue out and shook her head as if she was the one
who drank the tea on screen.

Reinhard and Wilhelm showed expressions of sympathy while the only
person who found that disgusting fact funny were the duo of chaos
Heinkel and Meili as they cackled for their own reasons due to Subaru's
misfortune.

"She really can't let anyone forget..." Al sighed while looking away,
not to any god this time, just tired already.

Subaru's parents both looked back to Anastasia, having their innocent
expressions to intensify what Kenichi had said from before, which
Anastasia glared at them for in the middle of her expression being
turned to one of disgust.

"All of a sudden, I'm veeeery glad not to have been invited for tea by
her..." Emilia muttered into her sleeve, holding on to Subaru's hand in
pity for him.

"Subaru-kun must not drink any bodily fluids without prior notice!" Rem
huffed with a small blush on her cheeks.

"I think... You need to be more disgusted than outraged by the optics
there... Rem..." Crusch blinked at the maid's skewed sense of priority.

"Retribution for Barusu's stupidity is surely fulfilling to watch," Ram
was highly pleased that Subaru got consequences this disgusting for his
recklessness.

*All the Witch did in reply was giggle like a mischievous schoolgirl.*

"*You truly are a fascinating individual." Echidna held a dark gaze that
was laid-back but interested, amused, et calculating. "You're standing
right here in front of me as if it's normal."*

*Subaru had to give a small chuckle at that.*

"*Hah! You think you're so pretty that a normal person's eyes would fall
out? I'll have you know that my eyes are blessed throughout the day by
the prettiest girl alive! So no matter how many times I look at you, I'm
not going to see you as particularly pretty."*

"Subaru..." Emilia let out a cute squeak, feeling her cheeks redden so
she covered her face by letting her silver hair fall down and leaned
away from everyone. She had a kind smile and found it easier to meet
Naoko's eyes.

The mother held a small smile for the half-elf as well, seeing a bit
more clearly now that her son was speaking about his crush in front of
another woman. "I never thought I'd hear him say that about a girl
before my hair went gray..."

"Guess we're lucky..." Kenichi found a bit of dark levity in their
situation as he grinned at his wife. "We get to see it in full HD,
babe."

Naoko sighed, not at all willing to laugh at that but still willing to
lean into her husband some more. "At least he's as terrible at
complimenting girls as his old man." She teased softly while her husband
flexed his shoulder muscles so she could lean on him more.

"That he is!"

While the parents were having their small moment, Rem was behind
everyone looking away from the screen with a little eye roll. Surprising
Crusch by showing a more openly jealous attitude.

"It's never easy to hear your crush say that about another woman,
Crusch-sama." Felix noted with a hint of worry for his lady, watching
her carefully.

Crusch shrugged and patted Rem's shoulder before looking back at the
screen, not at all affected.

"Aren't you... going to be sad that he's not complimenting you?" Felix
asked which made the duchess raise her brow.

"Why would I? He's already told me that he finds me suitable for the
eyes when he was in our care." Crusch's reply was calm and logical. Not
at all affectionate or emotional like Felix wanted it to be.

"N-Nevermind then... Nya..."

*Echidna didn't even react to such an assault on her beauty, merely
smiling at Subaru innocently. "No, most normal people vomit when they
stand before me. Funny, right?"*

*Subaru's hands slam on the table as he glares at her. "There's nothing
funny about that!"*

*But she only laughs in sheer delight.*

"She really never smiled or giggled like this with me..." Emilia found
herself looking more curiously at the Witch. "Does she like Subaru
already?"

"She's just a construct, I suppose." Beatrice sighed, though her eyes
seemed hopeful as well, like a cat that's missing affection. "I never
thought I'd see Mother giggle like a maiden again."

"What a nasty fucking laugh, Teacher." Al commented dryly from the back
though he didn't let anyone hear him. "Trying to play your part well...
Or maybe Pal really does produce such human feelings from you... Hm..."

While the knight was contemplating, Roswaal was all but glaring at the
big screen with as much hatred as he possibly could.

"*What is it you want with me?" Subaru glares at her with more
scrutiny.*

"*Nothing really," Echidna's smile relaxes to reveal more of her
calculating expression of interest. "You came in here of your own
accord. If you want to go back, I'll send you back..."*

*She gains a dangerous white gleam inside of her abyss-like irises, "But
are you sure that's what you want?"*

"*An opportunity to talk to the Witch of Greed is not something that
just anyone, other than you, can earn." Her voice was smooth in
delivery, perfect on the ears as she spoke with honeyed wording.*

"Everytime she says that title as if she owns it... A vein within me
ruptures..." Roswaal spits, moving to turn his head away and uncrossing
his legs for the first time since the previous viewing, Ram wondered if
he ever felt numb sitting like that.

"You truly do despise her... Does she act like your Teacher?" Ram tilted
her head, curious.

Roswaal bristles at the very notion. "Never assume such a stupid thing
again," he hisses hatefully. "My Teacher is far beyond the need to act
this animated to manipulate others into thinking she's human!"

"So Barusu's charm's not to blame for how 'favorable' she's trying to be
to him?" Ram sighed and turned from her agitated master.

"Not at all... Subaru-kun is very much deserving of her attention even
if she's an algorithm construct imitating my Teacher's soul." Roswaal
let go of his hateful expression and sighed, going back to leaning back
in his seat. "I didn't... Expect the construct to be so comfortable
however..."

"You never got the chance to meet with the replica of your teacher's
soul..." Ram noted without emotion, knowing how much that burns and
stabs at the man's heart.

She could only look away and hum to herself, smiling slightly when he
selfishly put a trembling hand on hers.

"*The only requirement to have a dialogue is for both of us to exist.
Let's remove everything unnecessary from the picture." Echidna's grin
grew at the edges as the table, umbrella, teacups, and pot all erased
themselves from existence.*

*Soon, the grass field around them both also began to disappear, eating
itself to become vast emptiness and slowly racing towards Subaru and the
Witch.*

"*Let me affirm all the desires, the curiosities, the greed, that you
seek to satisfy with answers."*

*Above them, the cloudy blue skies began to crack in ugly deformation as
if they were arctic ice swimming through the ocean. Only the ocean
waters were a dark, ugly combination of rainbow colors instead of the
sweet blue.*

*Subaru and Echidna remained in the middle of a world that was eating
itself and a sky that was breaking like glass. Staring at one another
head-to-head until the world around them turned into a painting out of
cosmic fantasy.*

*Colors of the aurora borealis mixed with the magnificent lights of a
thousand stars replaced the ruined fantasy world around them both.*

Kenichi gripped the arms of his chair so hard the material groaned. He
had seen planetariums in Japan, and he had seen the clear night sky on
camping trips, but this... this was like staring into the eye of God.

"It's pretty..." Naoko whispered next to her husband, making him unable
to do anything but look along at such a sight.

"The mana needed to conduct such an alteration of reality..." Reinhard
muttered into his gloved hand, looking beyond impressed by the woman on
the screen.

"... That's a witch for you." Julius glared at the colors on the screen,
remembering his own spirits. "How could someone with such magical
abilities escape the records---"

"Fuck the records," Heinkel snorted from above the knights. "Can't ya
see that whenever this kid deals with shit like this, he'll always have
otherworldly shit surrounding 'em without fault?"

"What a thing to hear from the so called deputy commander of the royal
guard, nya..." Felix shook his head but noticed that Crusch was eyeing
Heinkel with a certain tinge of respect.

"He's correct in that Subaru would be the only one to have someone
capable of this much power meet him within just months of his stay in
our world." Crusch analyzed the void around Subaru and Echidna with a
hint of awe clear on her usually steeled expression. "How beautiful..."

"*Now, what would you like to ask me?" Echidna spoke with confidence and
self-satisfaction as Subaru gaped at her inside this dark, yet
beautiful, world.*

"... Could ya imagine the sheer knowledge someone like this has?"
Anastasia mused with clear interest in her widened eyes. Her hands were
both placed upon the blue scarf that's never left her neck since the
start of the theater's imprisonment of their souls.

"Who'd waste such a grand prize as this?"

"M-My lady... You're sounding a little bit unhinged." Hetaro commented
from the side, showcasing worry.

"He's right, you don't look natural, Anastasia-sama. Is everything
okay?" Tivey affirmed his brother's worry with his own analytical
criticism. "How come you're not worrying about Natsuki-sama?"

"Oh hush," Anastasia waved a hand at the twins. "Ya both need t' learn
somethin' from yer sister. There's nothing like /neglect/ happenin'."

"Who said anything about neglect?" Tivey's eyes narrowed at his lady,
which earned him a couple of giggles from Anastasia.

Clearly, she wasn't taking him, or the situation itself any seriously
than she felt the need to.

"I can't help but feel a bit uneasy about all of this," Hetaro commented
with his brother nodding along.

"Oi, let's just watch what's happenin', eh?" Ricardo patted both boys on
their heads, winking towards his lady and looking back up. "Boss' got a
plan for the guy. Let 'er enjoy this."

"*About the Witch of Gluttony, Daphne," she begins to explain, as a
large dark cloud swims past Subaru's head, forcing him to look up as it
forms shapes of unknown origins above him.*

"*Who created beasts that defied the will of God to save the world from
starvation?" A black host of yellow eyes showed itself, but the screen
moved to show Echidna's face, smiling in the colorful abyss as she
continued.*

"Created...? Huh?" Julius, who'd been noting down things since the
beginning on a small notebook he'd borrowed from Tivey, suddenly blinked
out of his trance of writing to look at Reinhard.

"But..." Reinhard found himself in awe with the notion, afraid to even
speak it.

"I thought the Witch of Envy made the Mabeasts roam around to hurt us."
Schult mumbled while looking around.

"Whaaaat? She's the mama of all my beasties?" Meili smirked in delight
as she leaned forward. "Finally, this gets exciting!"

"Why is this information suddenly being given to Natsuki-san so freely?"
Otto immediately frowned. "How come you don't share any of this
information with the world outside the Margrave-san's territories?"

The closest Otto could ask was the girl sitting on Subaru's lap, which
forced Subaru's parents to be privy to this conversation as they were
sitting between them.

"Mabeasts... Was there supposed to be another witch that made them such
things?" Kenichi asked politely, not knowing what was happening or why
they were all focusing on another witch than Echidna.

"It's improbable for you to blame Betty for anything regarding the flow
of information when she's been guarding the greatest library of
knowledge for far longer than your ancestors were conceived, in fact."
Beatrice crossed her little arms and glared back at Otto, fully taking
offense.

Kenichi of course, was ignored, and he couldn't help but feel a little
bit of an insult at that but a small pat on his back surprised him.

He glanced back and saw the large beastman, Ricardo, giving him a thumbs
up and a grin. A notion of camaraderie which wasn't unwelcome by any
means. Kenichi seemed to respect this person more every time they
interacted.

"*About the Witch of Lust, Carmilla, who granted emotions to non-human
beings in an attempt to fill the world with love?" The shadow weaved
itself throughout the abyss to form the image of a silhouetted figure
with a large scarf flying behind their back.*

"That sounds so... Sad..." Naoko commented with her eyes showing
immediate sympathy for the woman.

"They don't sound like witches at all..." Emilia whispered under her
breath, receiving several nods of agreement from around her.

"What a kind heart..." Rem mumbled, biting her lip as she looked at the
scarf of the so called witch of Lust with sympathy. "I hope she didn't
regret such a decision." *\\
"About the Witch of Wrath, Minerva, who struck people to heal them as
she lamented the state of her war-torn world?" The shadow moved to
become the shape of a heroic woman with a ponytail that flew around the
dark abyss.*

Emilia's eyes widened in recognition with her smile being hidden behind
her hair and hand. "Ah... That's who you were..."

While she was recognizing Minerva, the others reacted appropriately to
what was being revealed in such tiny margins.

"I... Have never heard of such a being or Witch ever... That isn't even
worthy of the title..." Julius looked down at what he was noting neatly
upon his paper. "I find myself in more confusion than anything as to why
these women are being condemned."

"Healing the world... By striking it?" Petra looked more interested by
the tale being brought to her attention than anyone else in the room.
"That... It sounds like a wonderful thing... Why would she be a witch
for that?"

Crusch felt her arm being given a gentle squeeze, looking to her side,
she saw that Felix was all by glaring at the screen.

"Does the concept of healing the world anger you, Ferris?" She would ask
her knight with a raised brow.

The greatest healer in the kingdom shook his head. "No one would
disagree with the concept... I just... Ferri-chan doesn't think it's
fair to call someone a witch for healing... It's stupid, nya."

Her smile was small but clearly proud as she patted his head in
affirmation of his frustrations.

"*About the Witch of Sloth, Sekhmet, who drove a dragon past the great
waterfall just for a chance to rest?" The shadow formed a woman who was
lying down with a sinister glow within the area where the eyes would've
been, had this not been a black shadow forming shapes and monsters.*

"Damn," Ricardo blew a whistle as people around the room widened their
eyes at that revelation.

"A dragon? Not a ground one?" Kenichi asked, looking at Otto since he
was the closest surprised person to him and Naoko.

"Dragons that fly are a lot more prideful and chaotic... They are
ancient beasts with no power to tame them." Otto explained to the
parents with his appealing charm, understanding in his eyes that they
would need such history for they are not of this world.

"What's the great waterfall?" Naoko wanders from Kenichi's side. "This
feels nice to learn. It really does feel like we are watching a woman
teach the history of the world."

"Too much history to be honest," Tivey muttered with his eyes closing in
tiredness from how much he'd widened them the past minute alone.

"The great waterfall is the edge of our realm, I suppose." Beatrice

"Our world? Your 'realm' is just flat earth then?" Kenichi had a baffled

"Yours isn't like that, Natsuki-san?" Otto followed up with his own

since everyone was interested.

"Subaru never told us about this stuff... Well... Other than Beako."
Emilia tried to say kindly, leaning a little bit over Subaru so she

"Hmph! Try not to sound too jealous, in fact." Beatrice didn't try to

Emilia pouted and pulled on a drill from the spirit, something she

While Beatrice was yelping cutely on Subaru's lap, Kenichi shook his
head with a smile. "He really doesn't take the chance to show his

Anastasia couldn't help but affirm with a small hum and a grin while the

"Well... Our world is essentially a sphere. Like a round globe of sorts

"We wouldn't do such nonsense," Crusch chuckled. "Still, this is really

"I had thought it a bore to hear of past dead wenches but it seems like

"Kenichi... Do you think..." Naoko held on to her husband's sleeve and

Obviously all eyes were on her, including her husband's.

"Do you think um... That the dinosaurs and dragons in our world..."
Naoko gestured towards the sleeping ground dragon in the room, as if to

"Huh... No way... Oh my god!" Kenichi blinked as he thrust his chest

"What is a dinosaur?" Meili tilted her head, receiving a glare from

"It's an old reptile that became extinct within the borders, or shall I

"Betty's Subaru told her they have a large similarity to the ground
dragons in ours."

"They became extinct? How come?" Reinhard found himself more interested

"Are ya feelin' pity for 'em?" Felt raised an eyebrow, lazing against

"... A little. It's a sad thing to hear about the extermination of

"Ain't ya involved with exterminating various clans and races of

"Why did you say that so formally as if you're not a deputy commander of

"Gotta make 'em know what countries do what." Heinkel shrugged while

Schult gave him a small grin, seeing him being thoughtful for Subaru's

"Natsuki-san," Otto chuckled a bit. "Everyone's clearly interested in

Kenichi stopped his silence and looked away from Patrasche. "Well...

Beatrice kicked at Subaru's chin with the back of her heel as she

"Sorry! But..." Kenichi held a small chuckle in his throat. "Sorry...
Yeah. Well... It may be that your theory about what's beyond your

"How come?" Julius found himself too interested not to urge from behind

"Well," Kenichi looked back at Patrasche and over at the scene paused on

Many in the audience had wonder and intrigue on their faces at the idea.

"Could be that we had your dragons and 'dinosaurs'... The opposite idea

"Such a great deal for speculation." Roswaal sighed while glancing away

"But that would imply a lot of these dinosaurs were moved into your

"He isn't," Rem whispered while glancing at Subaru. "He's not alone in

Kenichi and Naoko both looked like a train was speeding toward them in a

"That's true..." Otto's eyes softened as he looked back. "We have

The parents looked back where Otto, and now seemingly the entire theater

Towards the man wearing a helmet.

"Suddenly having all this attention on me makes me feel like I should've

Pal-san's folks! Nice to formally meet ya."

"You..." Kenichi swallowed, not helping the shocked undertone he was

"Yeah... I was a bit younger than pal over there so I had... Lost more

"Sweetie..." Naoko didn't know why she was compelled to say that but she

It was clear to all the emotion on the mother's face wasn't for the

Al lets out a chuckle as he usually does. "It's fine, Mother-san." He

"I'm more than fine with how things are right now. I have a princess...
And I... I don't want to remember what I'm missing from home. You're

"Subaru... Forgetting where he came from..." Emilia muttered with her

"Don't worry about me." Al's voice repeated over the room. It was a

Either way... The parents looked over at the man with a hint of

"Al-san," Naoko gave a sad little smile towards the man, "if it would be

"That's a fantastic idea," Kenichi chuckled and looked at Al with a

"Saitama... " Al muttered.

The parents looked far more pleased than they wanted to be. "That's

"Yeah... It's where my parents are from as well." Al's voice was

"You really want to have me over sometime?"

"Of course... You're always welcome at my household." Kenichi extended

Al couldn't stop the slight stammer in his voice. "I... Uh... Sure... If

Kenichi grinned at Priscilla with a twinkle in his eyes, feeling happy

Priscilla herself simply offered the man an annoyed raise of her

one thing Priscilla appreciated was the polite remembrance of her
ownership over Al.

"Hm... Commoners with manners."

"This has been very touching..." Julius gave Al a slight nod of respect,
hoping the man got some closure with this new friendship Kenichi and
Naoko were offering him. "But I would like to continue watching what

A small wave of agreement sounded as Otto nodded his head to the

turning around, holding on to Subaru's hand.

It was only Crusch that held on to a frown when looking at Al. Her eyes
squinted slightly as she turned back to the screen and gripped her arm
rest.

She didn't know if Reinhard was activating his own divine protection of
the Wind Reading. But hers was always on.

And the wind around Al was nothing short of chaotic.

He was lying to them. He was lying to all of them about something... And
Crusch was the only one who knew.

She looked at them. Seeing how Kenichi was holding on to his wife with a
small smile of comfort and even encouragement.

The duchess closed her eyes and let out a tired breath. She didn't want
to take this away from the parents who'd been constantly pushed to the
side within the theater because of their isolation as other worldly
guests.

It was one comfort that she can't be cruel enough to take away from
them.

So Crusch... Stayed silent. And let Al be free of her intense scrutiny
for the moment.

The screen played the next scene with its light breaking over the black
helmet on Al's head as he sat in the quiet with nothing to say to

Even when Heinkel snorted and grunted in annoyance, "Fuckin' immigrant

And Schult was looking at him with something akin to awe and amazement.

But Al didn't focus on any of it.

He merely looked up to see Echidna's face. He felt a thousand emotions

Then he looked at Subaru... And he found only one emotion that tore
through his heart and soul with one stab.

"Princess...? May I please ask for something?" Al turned to the only

"..." Priscilla didn't interrupt him, she didn't even acknowledge him as

"Those mean nothing when your divine is awaiting your determination to
win, Aldebaran." Priscilla held a small grin as her hand slithered into

warmly.

Al... squeezed back as his eyes rolled up to the screen, feeling none of
the thousand emotions because of Echidna's face that he did before.

Watching through the tears that streamed down his face inside his
helmet.

*But Echidna would continue. "About the Witch of Pride, Typhon, who, out
of youthful innocence and cruelty, judged criminals one after another?"
The shadow formed the form of a small, innocent girl, paused in a twirl
forever.*

"A little girl?" Wilhelm raised an eyebrow with judgment in his eyes.
"How could a youth determine the fate of someone... and call it
justice?"

"Aren't I a youth that's been makin' yer son kneel for my judgment for a
while?" Felt raised an eyebrow towards Wilhelm with skepticism.

The old man didn't have a retort to say to that as he was left to look
at the wall with a deadpanned stare, realizing the faultiness of his
logic.

"My apologies."

Reinhard and Heinkel couldn't help the snorts that had to be hidden
behind gloved hands.

*And as the camera zoomed in on Subaru's trembling face, it clearly

"*About the Witch of Greed, Echidna," she continued without pause, "the

The screen zooms from Subaru's horrified expression to Echidna's much
more sinister one, darkness covering her eyelids.*

*Subaru gasps as her words begin to take effect on him.*

Roswaal's eyes narrowed, his gaze to the floor as he couldn't bear to
look at Echidna. "How cruel of you to say such things about my Teacher."

Al couldn't help but turn his eyes to Roswaal with his tears continuing
to stream. Without the helmet, Al would've been able to show how
unblinking his eyes were as they gazed upon Roswaal.

The hand on his squeezed tighter as his lady nudged him to turn back to
the screen.

"Shhh..." Priscilla clearly knew more than she showed as she calmed him
from the fury that filled his heart.

Fury because Al agreed with Roswaal's statement.

Beatrice was the only one who looked at Echidna with a hint of agreement

"*Or... The Witch of Envy, who killed all the other witches and used
them as sustenance while she made the whole world her enemy--- the most

*Subaru's face contorted into something inhuman, filled with despair and

The warm and even friendly atmosphere became null with the little scenes

The parents themselves winced at the terrifying description given to

"She consumed the souls of the witches?" Julius let his jaw hang in

"W-Was that Subaru... Interacting with her?" Kenichi asked, gulping at

"More so... She was interacting with him, Natsuki-san." Crusch responded

"Why...?" Kenichi turned to her with his eyes showing nothing but worry.

Naoko looked towards Emilia as her husband's question hung in the air.

The half-elf seemed to increase the grip on her son's arm as the light

body.

She looked like a girl possessed at the mention of the Witch alone.

"That's why we are watching your son's journey... To understand what she

Kenichi's eyes widened even more, and his expression showed heartbreak

"Holy shit..." He whispered with clear pity and sympathy for his son.

*With a snap, the dark world of abyss and colors cracked out of

"*Oh, darn." Echidna's tone suddenly returns to its innocent, girlish

*Subaru seemed to be shaking in place.*

"*Such is the nature of a witch. It's quite bothersome." She held a

"Mother is the only one to overwhelm with knowledge and act like it's an

Meanwhile, behind them, Rem moved a little closer to Subaru's seat,

"Barusu truly is unlucky..." Ram gave sincere sympathy from the back

Roswaal didn't give her any disagreement. He merely looked away from the

"Hmm," Meili was swaying from side to side while trying to see through

"Onii-san really has been up to no good before we met... Hm..." Meili

*Suddenly, a sound blared, which indicated something was changing within

*And immediately his face began twisting in a pained expression, holding

"*Oh, that took less time than I thought," Echidna spoke while holding

"*What are you talking about?" Subaru squeezed out while holding his

"*That tea you drank? I used the Envy Witch Factor on it to strengthen

"... Envy?" Julius had a look of disoriented dread that overtook his

More people were realizing what was just being said to Subaru.

"Isn't this the Factor thing that Beatrice-sama sent him to the Witch

"... Envy Witch Factor?" Emilia looked from the screen to Beatrice,

The spirit sighed tiredly, leaning more against Subaru as she recognized

"I can't explain what she's doing, I suppose... I can only speculate

"That's all we ask... We really want to understand what might happen

*Echidna leaned forward, her hands clasping under her chin as she

"*You won't get away with this!" Subaru growled at her. "What did you do

"*Please don't get the wrong idea. I didn't make you drink that tea

Echidna's smile turns cute as she looks up at him with her chin still

*She held her cheek while leaning back, a sheepish smile to replace her

*Subaru seemed to step back because of this, a confused expression

"... I don't like how she looks at my son..." Naoko shivered while

"What does she want with him?" Rem growled with her eyes narrowed with

"T-To have Mother act in such a way is deplorable, in fact." Beatrice

"M-Mate? Hold on... I thought Echidna was just being nice to him."
Emilia looked between the screen and Beatrice with a small blush on her

"... Fucking Witch..." Al muttered under his breath. "Goddamn it
Teacher... God fucking... Everyone chooses him in the end..."

"It's no wonder a construct with no chance of life could be interested

"How could it not be interested with the sheer ability he holds?"

Ram hummed softly, looking at her master with a hint of amusement.
Loving that he looked jealous even if he hated the Echidna on screen and

"What a man you are, master." Ram muttered, crossing her legs once
again.

"*In the past few days, you killed the one who possessed the Sloth Witch
Factor, correct?" Echidna asked Subaru, immediately entering her teacher
mode.*

*(LN Cut Content)*

"*So you're saying...the Witch Factor is the condition for being invited
into this castle of dreams?" Subaru asked.*

"*No, it is merely what qualified you to enter the tomb itself. You are
an exception among exceptions. To begin with, you seem to have leaped
quite a bit ahead of schedule---by rights, you should be aware of far
more than you are...about me, about the tomb, and about the Sanctuary."*

*Subaru stayed silent, thinking about what she'd just said to him while

"How did she know about what happened with Petelguese?" Otto asked, not
looking back since he didn't suppose anyone had an answer.

"I suppose the archbishops have this witch factor of their name sake...
The one Beatrice-sama was talking about in the previous viewing was
Sloth after..." Reinhard couldn't help but give Beatrice a small
sympathetic frown.

"After what Guese had become..." Emilia whispered for Beatrice's sake,

The spirit shook her head because she wasn't giving the thought of her
past friend the time of day. The spirit looked focused.

"Ahead of schedule?" Anastasia tilted her head as rubbed at her scarf.
"Schedule? Natsuki-kun's visit was scheduled?"

Eyes started raising in wonder as things began getting pointed to the
audiences.

"His visit to this place was... Planned out?" Kenichi raised his

creepy..."

Beside the parents, Otto held on to the pocket of his coat, feeling the

Roswaal gave no reaction to the comment by Echidna for anyone.

"*When he died, the Witch Factor chose you as its new dwelling. That's

"*Graveyard? Like... a tomb?" Subaru asked her, catching on suddenly.*

"*Yes." She answers bemusedly. "This is the Witch's Graveyard, where my
soul is held prisoner..."*

"*The Sanctuary."*

"... That explains why she's alive and drinkin' fuckin' body fluid tea,"
Heinkel grunted at the revelation. It didn't seem to hit anyone in the

"A grave... That's what the Margrave's family has been hiding from the

The knight of all knights had a look of pure dread and horror.

*As soon as Echidna finished what she was saying, Subaru's hands found

"*You know where the Sanctuary is?!"*

*Echidna could only look at him with bewilderment and confusion as he

"Very bold is the winner for sure," Frederica seemed to be taking

Nearly every girl had a small grin on their faces, especially Crusch,

Kenichi and Naoko tried to act like they were innocent of this behavior

Yes... Truly. These two were made for one another.

"*Don't make fun of me!" Subaru yelled, leaning down closer to the lady

"*So can I consider this... rather, the forest with the ruins where I

"*You're so mean... That's right. Just as you wanted, the Sanctuary is

"*So, what else would you like to as---" Echidna was cut off by Subaru,

"*And if I ask you, you'll let me back out, right?"*

*Echidna had to blink into space for a moment before glancing back at

*She leans forward slightly once she notices that Subaru's expression
has turned firm and resolved. "Hey, isn't there anything you want to ask

*He turned away with a stern face. "Sorry, but I'd rather meet with
Emilia outside than talk to you."*

"*Huh?" Echidna let out a girlish gasp, standing in her seat as Subaru
turned his back to her.*

"*Y-You're kidding, right?!" She waved her hands in the air, uselessly
as Subaru began walking away from her. "I'm the Witch of Greed!"*

*The screen shows Subaru walking away with an uninterested expression
while Echidna holds her arms together and waves her body unnaturally in
