search of my knowledge!"*

"Pfft---!"

The sound of Ricardo's booming laughter was the first to break the
stunned silence of the theater. He slapped his knee so hard it sounded
like a whip crack. "He's just walking away, treatin' the most powerful
woman in history like a stray cat askin' for scraps!"

"He really is a dunderhead," Emilia whispered, but her eyes were
sparkling. She couldn't help the small, triumphant smile that tugged at

the biting insults, and the general aura of hostility the Witch had
directed at her---seeing Subaru treat the same woman like an annoying
obstacle was incredibly cathartic.

"But... he wants to come back to you, Emilia-sama. He'd rather see you
than know the secrets of the world." Rem gave a chuckle.

The thought warmed Emilia more than any fire magic ever could.

"How dare he!" Beatrice shrieked, though she was bouncing slightly on
Subaru's lap. "Treating Mother with such utter lack of decorum, in fact!
To turn one's back on the Librarian of the World is a sin beyond
measure, I suppose!"

Despite her words of outrage, a tiny, rare giggle escaped her. Seeing
her mother---usually so poised and terrifyingly smart---reduced to a
stuttering mess by her Subaru was a sight she never knew she needed.

"This is something I didn't know I needed... Go on, Pal! Give 'er the

was practically vibrating in his seat, his single fist clenched and
resting on Priscilla's hand, squeezing it in his excitement. "That's how
ya do it! Don't let 'em lure ya in with the 'knowledge' trap! Stay
focused!"

Priscilla didn't pull her hand away. Instead, she watched the screen
with a look of supreme boredom that hid a flicker of genuine amusement.
"To see a Witch reduced to begging for the attention of a commoner...
What a sad pathetic thing."

In the back row, the sound that emerged from Roswaal was less of a laugh
and more of a wounded wheeze. The Margrave had his head buried in his
hands, his shoulders shaking.

"Master?" Ram asked, her voice cautious.

"It's... it's preposterous," Roswaal muttered, looking at the floor as a
hollow, defeated chuckle escaped him. "I have spent... so many
lifetimes... trying to reach her. Trying to hear her voice again. And
this construct chose to treat someone with interest for the first
time... And then he... Natsuki Subaru-kun... treats her like a common
nuisance."

He looked up, his mismatched eyes wide with a mixture of shock and a
strange, newfound respect. "She is offering him everything. And he
rejects it all for a promise of a morning greeting. She must've been
baffled... meeting the anomaly she cannot calculate."

"He's just an idiot," Ram summarized, though she looked pleased.

"SUBARU!" Naoko shouted at the screen, her face bright red. "Don't be so
rude! Even if she did make you drink... that... you should still say 'no
thank you'!"

"I don't think 'no thank you' covers turning down the secrets of the
universe, honey," Kenichi laughed, though he was wiping a tear of pride
from his eye. "Look at him go. He's got his priorities straight!"

"Is he... is he really just going to keep walking?" Felix asked, his jaw
literally hanging open. "Crusch-sama, that woman could probably tell us
how to cure... well, everything! And he's just... whistling?"

Crusch didn't answer immediately. She was too busy covering her mouth
with her glove, her eyes crinkled in a rare, genuine display of mirth.
"He is a man of singular focus, Ferris. Why would he need the secrets of
the world when he already knows everything that matters to him?"

"This isn't a joke!" Felix sputtered, trying to gain some seriousness in
the theater as Meili kept laughing just to piss him off on purpose. "He
could ask for a cure for your illness or Rem's!"

At this, some people stopped giggling and laughing from the Anastasia
and Emilia camps, though Heinkel in the back was one of the few who
couldn't find anything amusing in what was shown.

"... The cure to any illness..." Heinkel looked like a hollow man
without a soul as he stared at Echidna. "... Fuckin' bullshit... I swear
if he wasted this chance... I'll do what the clown told me to do..."

*She holds her arms out and looks at him with a panicked expression like
a saleswoman whose customer is walking away. "So, um... relax, okay?! We
can just relax and talk! I have knowledge about the truth of this world
that nobody---"*

*(LN cut content)*

*Subaru turns to face her with a calm expression. "You don't know about
things on the outside, right? It's tough saying this after all that
sound and fury, but to be honest, there's not really anything I want to
ask you... For example, that. Do you know details about the Witch Cult's
Archbishops of the Seven Deadly Sins?"*

"*Archbishops of the Seven Deadly Sins? Hmm, unfortunately, I do not
know the term. Could you tell me about them in more detail?" Echidna
scrambled.*

"Wait... she doesn't know?" Anastasia blurted out, her hands gripping
her scarf . "She's the Witch of Greed! The 'Thirst for Knowledge' or
somethin'! How can she not know about the folks who've been tearin' the
Kingdom apart for centuries?"

"Because she's a fossil," Julius answered, his voice a mix of
disappointment and stark realization. He adjusted his posture, his pen
hovering over his notebook. "If her death occurred before the sealing of
the Witch of Envy... that means the Witch's Cult is knowledge that would
require her to be alive for her to know."

"The lady who knows the secrets of the stars doesn't even know who's
currently kickin' the world's shins." Felt sighed with an eyeroll of
disappointment. "Goddamn fraud... She would make quick money in the
slums though."

"*Our positions really are reversed, sheesh... Well, if you don't know,
that's fine."*

*A smile came over Subaru at the sight of Echidna being thrown off---it
hid the pain in his chest.*

"*Yeah, it's fine."*

/*If she knew nothing of the archbishops, she knew nothing of their
Authorities, the damage they did, or ways to repair it.*/

---/*Meaning she didn't know how I could save Rem.*/

"Subaru-kun..." Rem whispered, her hand moving to her chest. She felt a
pang of guilt that even in a dream world with a Witch, Subaru's first
thought was to find a cure for her. To see that hope extinguished by
Echidna's ignorance was a fresh wound for her to witness.

Felix looked away from the screen with his face clearly showing shame
and guilt. "I'm sorry, nya..."

Crusch gave a sigh and patted his head once more, making him calm down.

"*HUH?!" Echidna slumped with both hands slammed on her elegant table,
jaw hanging open, clearly unaware of what to do in this situation. The
omniscient goddess was gone, replaced by a girl who had just been told
she was irrelevant.*

"*I'll make time to chat over tea with you later, okay?" Subaru tells
her, which makes Echidna slam her face into the table and stretch her
arms across it.*

*Her words are muffled against the table as she speaks. "You'd ask a
dead woman... a witch, at that... to agree to that as if it were the
simplest thing?"*

"Barusu has a talent for making the extraordinary feel mundane," Ram
noted, though her gaze stayed on the slumped figure of Echidna. "Even
Witches aren't immune to his unique brand of idiocy."

"Make time... For Echidna?" Al found himself looking up at the ceiling,
his earlier tears now dried. His breathing had calmed down a significant
amount but the pure tremble of happiness didn't stop from running
through his arm. "Fuck yes... Someone's putting ya in yer place you
nasty fuckin' Teacher."

Beside Ram, in the same row as Al, Roswaal could only sob into his hands
with sheer tenacity not to jump down and kill Subaru with his gloved
hands.

"... I feel a little bad for her. She seems lonely." Naoko couldn't help
but bite her lip a little bit.

Garfiel was the one to shake his head at the mother. "Believe me,
Cap'n's mother-san. She ain't worth a single fuck ya got to give."

"You met her before?" Kenichi asked the tiger, but Garfiel shook his
head again.

"Nah, but it ain't a good person who got this shithole of a Sanctuary
built around her tomb for. That's fer sure."

*Echidna kept her face hidden from him but waved her wrist, making a
loud noise that stretched behind Subaru, which made him turn around.*

*A large black hole opened through a tear in reality, like a rift of
darkness that shaped a portal for him.*

"*I've never had a tea party like this," Echidna speaks in a defeated
tone as Subaru takes a few steps toward the black portal.*

"*You're really going back... I'll just take my compensation first."
Echidna's voice turned cold, though she stayed in her position on the
table.*

*This made Subaru pause his steps, turning around with a slightly
sheepish expression. "J-Just so you know, I'm broke beyond compare..."*

"He's still joking," Felix muttered, his ears flat against his head.
"Doesn't he realize the atmosphere just dropped forty degrees?"

"He's trying to keep his footing," Crusch observed, her eyes narrowed.
"Humor is his only shield against that level of power."

*Echidna doesn't rise from the table as she explains to him.
"Compensation for a witch doesn't mean money."*

*She slowly stood up with a smooth smile, and her head was held high as
she looked at him. "What I desire from you is a vow."*

"A vow?" Emilia whispered, her hand tightening on Subaru's sleeve.
"That... that sounds like a contract. Beako, is she trying to bind him?"

"A Witch's Vow is no light matter, in fact!" Beatrice hissed, her drills
trembling. "It is a promise etched into the soul. To tell no one of the
tea party... it is a way to isolate his experiences, I suppose!"

"So... He couldn't tell us about this meeting..." Otto realized before
the rest of the Emilia camp. "... He was in the same position as the
Witch of Envy about his ability..."

"That explains why Cap'n never mentioned what happened after he got
outta those ruins..." Garfiel sighed, his eyes downcast, afraid of what
will be shown next.

Behind them, Julius didn't give any indication of interest, ignoring
both of them and wishing for the screen to continue.

*Like it was the simplest thing, she walked away from her table and
teacups towards him. "You will tell no one of what has transpired at
this tea party. That is my condition."*

"*And, since you're here, I'll send you away with a souvenir. I will
grant you the right to face the trial of the Sanctuary."*

"*The trail of the Sanctuary?" Subaru repeated with a confused face, not
knowing what that meant.*

*She raised her hand, opening her palm and placing it firmly on his
chest.*

"*You may not understand now, but once you learn where it is, you will
realize its worth."*

*Echidna's voice takes on a more giddy tone as she continues speaking.
"What feelings will you have toward me when that happens?"*

Emilia's breathing hitched as she looked at the ground with sudden
realization. "He... Got the key to the trails... From here?"

Beatrice and the others in the Emilia camp looked at the scene with a
hint of understanding and refused to look towards the half-elf. "It's
done already, in fact. You were the one who won the trials."

"The Sanctuary's trials?" Tivey was about to ask the Emilia camp but a
slight gesture from Hetaro and a shake of his head stopped him.

"What good are they as allies if we can't ask for anything?" Tivey
sighed and leaned back with his own notes scribbling.

"What good is explaining if we can just watch to get our answers?"
Julius countered from the side with elegance. Which was a good point.

Rem held a small frown at that, feeling very much like the outsider as
she didn't understand what the Emilia camp was going through yet was
being treated as one of them by the others in the theater.

*She moved her hand from his chest to lick at her fingertips like a
vampire tasting skin.*

"*The mere thought gives me such beautiful expectations." She licked
along her hand until she could look at Subaru, opening her eyes to
reveal that her dark black irises had turned dark crimson.*

"*You..." Subaru grinned, though it was a frightened sort of expression
for a grin. "You really are a witch, aren't you?"*

"*Yes, that's right," Echidna agreed readily with a step into his
personal space, which made Subaru step back. "I'm a very, very evil
magic user."*

"That... that filthy witch!" Rem's eyes were glowing with a blue light,
her horn practically itching to manifest. "How dare she look at him with
such... such depravity!"

*She moved her hand up and flicked Subaru's forehead.*

*He couldn't stop himself from falling backward, losing control of his
body as if it were a mere rock pile that had been pushed over by her
finger.*

*Subaru shouts as Echidna looks down at him from beyond the black portal
as the rift closes with Subaru in the darkness of the portal.*

*And it cuts to black.*

He's out," Otto breathed, wiping sweat from his forehead. "He's finally
out of that place."

"But at what cost?" Julius asked, looking at his empty notebook. "He
carries a vow he can't speak of and a 'right' to face trials that made
even a Witch look giddy. The Sanctuary... we're finally going to see
what happened there."

In the back, Al was slumped in his seat, his hand finally letting go of
Priscilla's. "Evil magic user, huh? Teacher... you really don't know how
to let a guy go without a scar."

Roswaal, however, remained silent. He stared at the black screen, his
expression a mask of complex, agonizing expectation. The script was back
on track, but the lead actor had already broken every rule of the stage.

*Slowly, Subaru's eyes open to find himself back in the ruins where he'd
been taken from.*

*He holds his hand to his ear, fixing himself while standing on the
mossy cobblestone below him and looking at the lit-up end of the
tunnel.*

"*Anyway, I need to get out of here and back to Emilia-tan and Otto."
His sneakers scuffed the stone as he walked out.*

/*I feel like...*/

*His eyes were staring at the floor below him with an anxious
expression.*

/*Someone said something to me.*/

*Subaru reaches the end of the tunnel, out in the open, where the sun
lights up his surprised expression as he sees the scene awaiting him in
the clearing before the ruins.*

*Otto was lying on his back, clothes scuffed all over, and his eyes in
swirls from the clear scuffing that he'd been subject to.*

"Otto-kun!"

The cry didn't come from the screen, but from the theater itself. Emilia
was half-out of her seat, her eyes wide with a mixture of shock and a
growing, gnawing fear. "Why... why is he like that? Who did that to
him?"

She turned her head, her silver hair whipping around, looking for
answers from the merchant himself. But Otto was slumped so low in his
seat he was nearly invisible. His face was a shade of red that rivaled
Priscilla's dress, his hands covering his eyes.

"Please," Otto's voice came out as a strangled whimper. "Just... let the
screen go black again. I don't want to see this."

"You look like a bug that got stepped on, Mister Merchant," a
high-pitched, giggling voice drifted down from the row above.

Meili was leaning over the back of her chair, her chin resting on her
crossed arms. She poked a finger toward the screen, tracing the "swirls"
in Otto's eyes with a dark, childish glee. "Does your head feel like
it's full of bees? Onii-san looks so surprised to see you like this. I
wonder if you'll make a funny sound if he kicks you."

"Meili! That's horrible!" Petra snapped, though she too was looking at
the screen with intense worry.

"It's just a question," Meili hummed, her purple eyes shimmering with a
mischievous, misunderstood coldness. "When things get scuffed up, they
usually break. He's like a very sturdy weed."

While the girls bickered, a different kind of tension was radiating from
the blonde youth sitting near the front. Garfiel was vibrating. Not with
anger, but with a raw, jagged dread that made his fangs click together.

/This is it/, Garfiel thought, his claws digging into the plush
armrests. /This is where Cap'n and I meet.../

He looked at the screen, at the ruin of the merchant he'd just finished
"questioning", and then toward Subaru's sleeping face. The fear of being
rejected by the only man he called "Captain" was a weight heavier than
any stone in the Sanctuary.

"Garfiel?" Frederica whispered, reaching out a hand to her brother. He
flinched away, his gaze locked on the display.

"Shut it," Garfiel growled, though his voice lacked any bite. It was the
sound of a boy waiting for a sentence to be passed.

"It looks like he's been through a tumble dryer," Kenichi noted, trying
to inject some levity into the room, though his eyes were sharp. "He
looks... well, he looks like he met a wall. A very fast-moving wall."

"It was no wall, Natsuki-san," Julius said, his eyes narrowing as he
looked from the screen to Garfiel's rigid back. "That is the work of a
predator. Someone wasn't happy about Subaru entering those ruins."

"*Otto?!" Subaru exclaimed before another gruff voice cut in.*

"*Look at you, just waltzing out of there! You've got some nerve,
outsider!" The voice was filled with loud and obnoxious revelry as
Subaru was suddenly held up by someone who had snuck behind him.*

"*Is this like a magmarin laughing when it's full of holes?!" Sharp
teeth are shown, owned by the person holding Subaru up like a sack of
potatoes, before he was sent to fly high in the sky by a mere throw.*

"SUBARU!" Naoko gripped her seat.

"Oh, look at him fly!" Meili giggled, her eyes wide with genuine
amusement. "Do you think he'll bounce, or just go 'splat' like a
overripe melon? I always liked the 'splat' sound best."

"Meili, stop it!" Petra cried, but the assassin girl only leaned further
forward, watching the trajectory with clinical interest.

*Subaru screamed through the air in the manliest way possible from the
great MC of this story. So manly, in fact, that it summoned his ground
dragoness from her great slumber to screech and run with the carriage
attached to her, following suit.*

*Patrasche did a sharp turn with her body moving in such a way as to
force the carriage to physically drift in the dirt and align itself in a
way that Subaru's fall is broken by the ceiling of the fancy cart before
he falls back down on the leathered seating for the drivers.*

*As Subaru was getting his bearings together from his fall, Patrasche
faced the sharp-toothed person who dared to commit such a heretical
policy of violence on her rider.*

"I don't... I don't understand," Emilia whispered, her voice trembling.
She looked back at the screen, her brow furrowed in a deep, agonizing
confusion. "I was with Subaru. We went into the woods together. I should
have been right there when he came out... but I don't remember this."

"YES!" Kenichi suddenly stood up, his fist pumping the air with a roar
of triumph that startled everyone. "THAT'S MY GIRL! Did you see that?!
That was a professional-grade catch! Patrasche-chan, you miracle of
nature!"

He didn't just look at the screen, he turned around to look at the
physical Patrasche, who was still curled up in a corner of the theater,
her chest rising and falling in deep, unconscious breaths. Kenichi
rushed over to the sleeping dragoness, regardless of the tension in the
room, and began doting on her with abandon.

"Who's the best girl? Who's the smartest, most heroic lizard in two
worlds? You are!" Kenichi cooed, patting her snout and adjusting her
harness with the pride of a man whose daughter had just won a
championship. "Look at her, Naoko! Subaru doesn't deserve her, the lucky
brat!"

"She really is... remarkable," Crusch murmured, a hint of professional
envy in her eyes as she watched the dragoness on screen immediately
square up against Garfiel.

"*Damn it, what the hell?!" Subaru said in a pained growl, sitting
straighter on the driver's seat of the cart.*

*He quickly turns around to open the driver's window and lean into the
carriage to see inside. He finds Emilia's sleeping form inside,
perfectly placed straight on the carriage couch.*

"I was asleep?!" Emilia widened her eyes in remembrance and outrage.
"Oh... Oh that's when they met..." She looked to Garfiel with pity.

*Subaru breathed out in relief, gaining a small smile, before the
loud-voiced attacker started speaking.*

"*What a tantalizing choice!"*

*Subaru stands up on the carriage driver's spot, glaring alongside
Patrasche towards their attacker.*

*The blonde man with an X scar on his face grins, standing tall. "That's
a nice ground dragon ya got there."*

Garfiel, in the theater, couldn't look away. He saw himself on the
screen, the predator, the bully, and he saw the dragoness who had more
loyalty in her pinky claw than he had in his entire body at that moment.

"I'm sorry, Cap'n," Garfiel whispered into his hands, the guilt finally
breaking through his facade as the screen focused on Patrasche's bared
teeth and Subaru's trembling, grateful gaze.

*Patrasche screeched at the blonde man and began to charge toward him
while Subaru immediately held on to the carriage with increasing panic.
"Wait, Patrasche!"*

*But the dragoness had somehow disconnected herself from the carriage,
so Subaru now watched her dash to attack the strangely calm man on her
own.*

*Both Subaru and Otto looked on with bated breath as Patrasche moved to
bite the mere human who had caused her rider such distress.*

*But when she bit on his shoulder, the dragoness couldn't even make the
blonde man flinch as he stood in place without a problem to allowing the
dragon's mouth to bite into his upper shoulder.*

"*You're a real good-lookin' girl, ain't ya? I'm not gonna hurt ya!" The
blonde wrapped his arm around her long, scaly neck with his grin
growing.*

"*Just take a little nap!" He flipped Patrasche's entire body upside
down, keeping her mouth on his shoulder as he dropped her on her back.*

*Effectively, Patrasche was knocked unconscious with spit flowing from
her mouth onto the floor.*

"*Better thank her for fightin' so hard for ya!" The blonde glanced from
the scaly girl he'd just abused to smirk at Subaru and Otto.*

"*Wait... Wait!" Subaru pleaded, but the blonde attacker was already
striding towards them with swiftness.*

"*I'm gonna throw you guys outta this forest half-dead... Just alive
enough to swear you won't tell a soul about this place!" The stranger
dashed with one step, pulling himself up with a foot on the edge of the
carriage as a punch was aimed toward Subaru's cheek.*

The "thud" of the majestic Earth Dragon hitting the ground upside down,
spit flying from her mouth, echoed through the theater like a gunshot.

In the center of the front row, Kenichi Natsuki didn't move. He didn't
shout. He sat perfectly still, his chin resting on his interlaced
fingers. The dim light of the theater screen caught the lenses of his
glasses, turning them into two opaque white voids that completely masked
his eyes. The shadow of his brow fell heavily over the rest of his face.
It was the silent, terrifying stillness of a father.

A father who's watched best girl just be disrespected.

"That... that brute!" Petra's voice broke the silence, high-pitched and
vibrating with pure indignation. She stood up, her small fists shaking
at the screen. "Garf-san! You're a bully! To do that to Patrasche-sama
when she was only trying to protect Subaru-sama?! You need to
apologize!"

"Quiet, little sprout," Priscilla's voice cut through the air, smooth
and dangerously amused. She fanned herself slowly, her red eyes dancing
with a devious glint. "The beast was a fool to challenge a superior
predator."

"It was like watching a bug get swatted," Meili giggled, her feet
swaying with dark rhythm. "Poor wittle dwagon... she looked so silly
with her tongue hanging out."

"He's certainly got the technique for it," Heinkel snorted, though his
eyes were narrowed with a soldier's appraisal. "Kid didn't even brace
himself for the bite. That's either insane confidence or he's built like
a fortress. Probably both."

"Please, everyone!" Frederica stepped forward, her hands clasped in a
calming gesture, though her own eyes flickered with a hint of sisterly
concern for how the room was perceiving the blonde on screen.

"My brother... Garfiel... he isn't a monster. He is merely...
overzealous in his duties. He would never truly harm a creature as noble
as an Earth Dragon unless he felt the Sanctuary was in peril."

"Duties?" Roswaal's voice was a low, melodic drawl. He didn't even look
at the screen, instead tracing a pattern on the armrest of his chair. To
him, Garfiel wasn't a person, he was a piece on a board.

"He is exactly what he was meant to be. A sharp, albeit loud, instrument
to ensure the borders of the Sanctuary remain... properly respected.
Whether he is 'kind' is irrelevant as l~ooo~ng as he is effective."

Ram let out a long, weary sigh, her gaze fixed on the screen where the
blonde was now charging Subaru. "Master is right about one thing: he is
very loud. But Frederica is correct as well. Garfiel is a child playing
at being a villain. He knows exactly how much force to use to 'rough
them up' without causing permanent damage. He's showing off for an
audience that isn't there."

She paused, a small, knowing smirk touching her lips. "He's always been
prone to dramatics when he thinks he needs to be the 'strongest' in the
room. It's pathetic, really."

"It's more than pathetic, it's disappointing," Rem added, her tone
uncharacteristically cold.

She remembered the two times she had accompanied Roswaal to the
Sanctuary; she had seen Garfiel's strength, but to see it used against a
defenseless Otto and a confused Subaru made her grip her skirt.

"Subaru-kun is a guest. To treat a guest with such violence, even if it
is 'scaled back,' is a failure of hospitality. You haven't changed at
all, Garfiel-kun."

The man in question, the real Garfiel sitting in the theater, finally
couldn't take the mounting criticism anymore. He jumped to his feet, his
fangs bared in a grin that was half-pride and half-defensive snarl.

"Oi! Watch yer mouths!" Garfiel barked, gesturing wildly at the screen.
"Ya see that? I didn't draw a single drop o' blood! I caught 'er mouth
on my shoulder 'cause I knew my skin could take it, and I flipped 'er
soft! She's havin' a nice nap on the grass, not headin' to the butcher!"

He turned his gaze toward Petra and the parents, his chest puffed out.
"My amazin' self has honor! I ended that fight in three seconds so I
didn't have to hurt the girl! I was protectin' my home! Ya think I'm
gonna let some random carriage roll in without checkin' 'em?"

"You're still scaring them, you idiot," Otto muttered, though he looked
relieved that the "real" Garfiel was at least defending his intentions.

"Scarin' 'em is the point!" Garfiel retorted, though he looked a bit
more sheepish as he saw the light still reflecting off Kenichi's
glasses. "The 'Great Tiger' doesn't invite people in with a hug and a
bowl o' soup! He makes 'em swear to keep their traps shut!"

"*YOU KNOW FREDERICA, DON'T YOU?!" Subaru shouted with his eyes closed,
and every cell in his body clenched, effectively awaiting his fate to be
punched by the blonde, but gambled one last time before it was lights
out.*

*It worked...*

*Garfiel kept his hand frozen in the air as he looked down. "Why the
hell did I just hear that name from your nasty mouth?"*

*Subaru opened his eyes to glare up at the blonde. "Why do you think?
Just think about it!"*

"*Thought about it," Garfiel replied instantly, readying his fist with
an excited smile. "Don't know. I'll think after I bash your skull in!"*

"*Quit jumping to conclusions without thinking! It's because we know
her, too!" Subaru shouts rapidly, trying to appeal to the blonde before
any attack happens.*

"*Huh?" Garfiel lowers his fist and leans down to face Subaru's eyes
better. "I'm Garfiel. If you guys know her, you must've heard that name,
right?!"*

"*You're..." Subaru hesitated as the blonde above him glared.*

"*How the hell did you know I have anything to do with Frederica?!"*

*Subaru's face dropped into a deadpanned expression that was
contemplating life itself. "Are you being serious right now?"*

*Garfiel's sharp teeth glowed with a shine.*

On screen, Garfiel's confused demand brought a wave of deadpanned
expressions to the theater.

"Is he being serious right now?" Felt blurted out, her feet kicking the
air.

"He's always been... simple-minded," Ram sighed, though her eyes
softened as she watched the blonde youth on screen.

"Oi! Shut it!" Garfiel barked, though he avoided looking at Frederica.
"I was shocked! You don't just expect some random guy from the city to
walk in and know your sister! It's weird! It's suspicious! I was
protectin' her honor!"

"By almost punching her friend in the face?" Rem asked, her tone dry but
lacking its earlier frost. "You really are a handful, Garfiel-san."

"Whatever!" Garfiel sat back down, huffing. "Just watch the screen. The
Cap'n is about to see how amazing my amazing self truly is, even if he
is a bit of a smart-ass about the family tree."

The theater shifted into a lighter mood, the humor of Garfiel's density
providing a much-needed reprieve.

Yet, as Otto stole another glance at Frederica---and she caught him,
this time holding the gaze with a soft, playful twinkle in her eyes.

It was clear that while the screen was showing the start of a friendship
between Subaru and Garfiel, a different kind of bond was beginning to
form in the rows of the theater itself.

*The scene changes to show the carriage moving again with Patrasche and
Otto at the helm.*

"*I just got beaten up for nothing." Alone in the driver's seat, Otto
was muttering to himself with a frown of contemplation. "What am I
supposed to do with this anger?"*

*Garfiel's voice can be heard from within the carriage behind Otto. "You
never shut up, do ya, trader? I apologized, didn't I?"*

"*You call, 'Hey, sorry, numbskull, I kinda jumped the gun,' an
apology?!" Otto exclaimed from the outside of the carriage with fury.*

In the theater, the real Otto buried his face in his hands as a ripple
of laughter spread through the rows.

"My, Otto-kun," Anastasia chuckled, her eyes gleaming with mischief. "Ya
sure do have a lot of bark for someone who was just shakin' like a leaf
a minute ago. Is that how ya negotiate with your business partners?
Yellin' until your face turns the color of a ripe tomato?"

"It wasn't like that!" Otto protested, though he didn't uncover his
face. "Anyone would be angry after being flipped over by a demi-human
who hasn't learned the meaning of the word 'wait'!"

Meili giggled from her seat, looking at Otto like he's a fun toy.

"A merchant's only value is his tongue," Ram remarked, her tone carrying
that familiar, sharp edge that always managed to sound like a refined
insult. "And yet, Otto-kun, yours seems to have tangled itself into a
knot of useless, high-pitched rage. It's a miracle the carriage hasn't
veered off the road from the sheer weight of your wounded ego."

"You guys are all being incredibly unfair," Otto groaned, slumping back.

Beside him, Garfiel let out a sharp snort. He looked at the screen, then
at Frederica, before finally casting a sideways glance at Otto. The
defensive snarl he'd been wearing softened into something closer to a
begrudging respect.

"Fine, fine," Garfiel muttered, his voice uncharacteristically low. "The
'numbskull' bit was probably uncalled for. I guess... thanks for not
let'n my amazing self actually kill the Cap'n before we even got
started."

Otto blinked, the fire in his eyes dying down as he looked at the blonde
youth. He sighed, a long, weary sound that spoke of a man used to being
the universe's punching bag. "I suppose I can't stay angry forever if
you're actually going to look like a wet cat. Apology accepted,
Garf-san. Just... try to use your words before your fists next time?"

"Don't push your luck, Otto-bro," Garfiel grinned, flashing his fangs,
but the malice was gone.

*Inside the cart, Subaru was sitting on the floor with his head lowered
towards Emilia's sleeping form.*

*Ignoring Otto's ramblings from the outside, Garfiel is revealed,
leisurely sitting back with his arms behind his head.*

"*Anyway, if you'd shown me that thing sooner, that woulda done it."
Subaru turned to the blonde as Garfiel smirks easily. "Gotta act on a
ganglion before ya think about it."*

"*Gangli... huh?" Subaru looked confused, but the blonde continued.*

"*Lookin' at that hair, I'm guessin' that's the famous Emilia-sama? The
silver-haired half-devil that Roswaal's helpin' out."*

*Subaru stands up with his fists clenched immediately, while Garfiel
smirks with satisfaction. "She's a half-elf! Don't you ever call her
that again!"*

*The greatest ragebaiter this side of the Sanctuary hood bellows a
laugh. "Hey, look at that! Guess you can put some energy into your voice
after all!" He put his hands together and began cracking his knuckles as
a twisted expression of excitement overtook his relaxed one. "Wanna know
how it feels to be a bazomazo bein' flung back an' forth?"*

*But all Subaru could respond to this challenge with was bewilderment.
"Uh, is there some sort of translation glitch happening between you and
me?"*

"*Huh?"*

The theater itself went cold. The term 'half-devil' echoed through the
room like an accusation.

In the theater, the real Garfiel looked as though he wanted to
physically vanish. The crimson hue of his face deepened, spreading to
the tips of his ears.

He stared at his boots, the usual "amazin' self" nowhere to be found.

"I... the amazin' self really was a stupid brat," Garfiel muttered, his
voice uncharacteristically low and thick with shame. "Emilia-sama... I
shouldn't have said that. I was just tryin' to act tough and pick a
fight. I'm sorry."

The sincerity in his voice caused a few heads to turn in surprise.

Kenichi's protective posture softened, his jaw unclamping as he realized
the boy wasn't a monster but a child who had been defensive and
frightened.

Naoko, sensing the genuine weight of the boy's guilt, leaned over toward
his seat between her and Otto. Her eyes were filled with the same warmth
she gave Subaru after he'd made a mistake.

"It's alright, Garfiel-kun," she said softly, reaching out to give his
shoulder a comforting squeeze. "Sometimes, when we're scared, we use
mean words as a shield. You were just protecting your home, weren't you?
It takes a big person to admit they were wrong."

Garfiel blinked at her, momentarily stunned by the lack of the scolding
he expected. He looked like a cub that had accidentally bitten a hand
trying to feed it.

"Hehe... 'half-devil,' was it?"

The voice was light, musical, and entirely unexpected.

Emilia didn't look hurt. Instead, she was looking at Garfiel with a
playful, mischievous glint in her eyes that made even Beatrice look up
in shock.

She didn't let go of Subaru's hand---she pulled it slightly closer,
resting her other hand over his as she addressed the blonde demi-human.

"My, Garfiel," Emilia teased, her tone ringing with a newfound
confidence and assertiveness that left several people, including
Anastasia, gaping. "I didn't know you thought I was so 'famous'. Though,
if I'm a half-devil, does that make you a quarter-monster? Or perhaps
just a very grumpy little kitten?"

"H-Huh?!" Garfiel stammered, his eyes widening.

"And all that talk about 'ganglions' and 'bazomazos'..." Emilia
continued, her grin widening as she leaned forward. "Don't you know that
no one uses such a weird way of speaking? Manners should be reeeeally
well respected by someone who cares about the knightly code you claim to
respect."

"I... mine amazin' selfs... I was just..." Garfiel was stumbling over
his words, his face now a shade of red that rivaled a ripe fruit.

"If you want a real apology accepted," Emilia chirped, her assertiveness
clearly catching him off balance, "you have to say it properly. No weird
metaphors about ganglions. Just... 'Emilia-sama, I'm sorry for being a
big, silly meany-pants.'"

A ripple of genuine laughter spread through the theater. Ricardo let out
a booming cackle, while even Julius hid a smile behind his hand.

"I... Fine!" Garfiel huffed, throwing his hands up in defeat, though the
defensive snarl was completely gone. "Emilia-sama... I'm sorry for
callin' ya that. And... and for the 'bazomazo' stuff. I'll try to use my
words better from now on."

"Much better," Emilia said, settling back into her seat with a satisfied
nod, her hand still firmly interlaced with Subaru's. "We'll work on your
vocabulary later. For now, let's see what other silly things you have to
say."

Garfiel couldn't believe the fall from grace he'd been subject to as
Emilia of all people criticized his way of speaking. The days of "who
says that in this day and age" were now in the past as Subaru was not
here to rule the Emilia camp's bullying roster.

"Cap'n's made a monster..." Garfiel muttered, face red.

*But before either could continue, Emilia began to stir from Subaru's
side.*

"*Emilia!" Subaru moved to kneel in front of her, waiting for her to
rise in her seat. He had a bright smile on his face while Garfiel stared
at them with a calm face. "I'm so glad you're okay!"*

"*Subaru?" Emilia looked at him and then gasped when she noticed Garfiel
in the carriage with them. She immediately moved forward, standing over
the kneeling Subaru and stretching her arms out to cover him, looking at
the blonde with resolve and protectiveness.*

"*Who are you?! Just so you know, I won't let you lay a finger on
Subaru!"*

"*Hang on, Emilia-tan!" Subaru stands up and holds his hands out to
Emilia with a sheepish expression. "I appreciate it, but as a guy, I
have mixed feelings about this! Really, I'm fine!"*

The laughter that this obtained could be a death sentence for any
person's reputation in the hood. Especially since it's Kenichi leading
the charge while Otto took the chance to enact the code of his
brotherhood with Subaru and laugh at his emasculation.

Everyone else, like Julius, Heinkel, and Felix, were just being petty.
While Hetaro just wanted to be involved and Schult wasn't allowed to
laugh by a glare from Priscilla and a small pat from Meili who was bored
to death.

Emilia, sitting in her theater seat, looked between the laughing men and
the screen with a look of pure, unadulterated bafflement. She didn't
understand the joke at all. Her hands were still tightly gripping
Subaru's sleeve, her brow furrowed in a cute, confused pout.

"But... why are they laughing?" Emilia asked, her voice small and
genuinely perplexed. "Garfiel looked dangerous! And Subaru was still
hurt from everything before. Why wouldn't I protect him? Is it... is it
wrong to want to keep him safe?"

"It's not wrong, Emilia-sama," Rem's voice cut through the laughter, but
it wasn't a comfort. The maid stood up slightly in her seat, her blue
eyes flashing with a competitive fire as she looked at the screen and
then back at the half-elf. "But you shouldn't feel like you have to do
it alone."

Rem turned her gaze toward the back of the room, her voice gaining a
sharp, formal edge. "For the record, I would also protect Subaru-kun
with my life. Whether he has 'mixed feelings' or not, I will be the
shield that stands before him!"

The theater exploded into a fresh wave of noise.

Felix was practically rolling in his seat, his ears twitching with every
laugh. "He's so worried about being the knight, but he's clearly the
princess of the camp! Subaru-kuyn needs all her guardians to keep the
mean monsters away!"

Amidst the chaos of the men's laughter and the girls' protective
declarations, a small, bright voice rang out from the row behind them.

"I think it's wonderful!"

Petra was leaning forward, her face glowing with delight. "Emilia-sama
looks so cool! To stand up like that... it proves how much she cares!
Subaru-sama is lucky to have someone who doesn't care about 'manly
feelings' when his safety is on the line!"

Emilia blinked, her confused expression softening as she looked back at
the little maid. "You... you think so, Petra?"

"I know so!" Petra nodded vigorously.

Naoko reached over and patted Emilia's hand, her giggles finally
subsiding into a gentle, knowing smile. "Don't mind them, Emilia-chan.
Men are very silly about their pride. But as a mother, seeing you stand
up for my son like that... it makes me very, very happy. You're exactly
the kind of girl I hoped he'd find."

Emilia's face turned a shade of pink that rivaled the dawn, her silver
hair falling forward as she looked down at Subaru's hand.

"*I'm not gonna do anything," Garfiel says while leaning his head back
into his hand and grinning with closed eyes. "I don't want Ram givin' me
hell later."*

"*Ram?" Emilia blinked in surprise, lowering her protective arms while
Subaru gave her a smile of assurance.*

"*This is Garfiel."*

"*Garfiel... The one Frederica told us about?" Emilia looked down at the
relaxed blonde boy with curiosity.*

"*Yeah, and he's showing us the way to the Sanctuary now." Subaru glares
down at Garfiel, taking a more stern tone. "Ram and the villagers from
Arlam are there... right?"*

"*Don't wanna believe me? That's your call." Garfiel opened his eyes to
give them a sinister grin. "Ain't no turnin' back now, though. We are
already past the barrier."*

*Emilia's eyes widened in recognition. "Then we're in the Sanctuary
right now?"*

"*C'mon, gimme a break," Garfiel calls out, making them stop looking
around uselessly. "Weren't you asleep 'cause you got close to the
barrier?"*

"*The barrier?" Emilia blinked. "Come to think of it, when the stone
glowed... It was at the same moment."*

*Subaru held the blue crystal given to them by Frederica in his palm and
looked at it.*

"*So it was reacting to the barrier, then?" Subaru asked Garfiel, but
the blonde only glared at the crystal in Subaru's hand. "But why did
only Emilia-tan pass out? I mean, I got transported somewhere, but
Otto---"*

*A loud scoff cut Subaru off and gained Emilia's attention.*

"*You know damn well why the barrier reacted to her and not you two." He
held an ugly grin on his face that affected Emilia.*

"*It's not just half-elves that thing reacts to. It works on me and a
lot of others, too. That's 'cause..."*

*When Garfiel trailed off, Emilia continued for him.*

"*Because you have mixed blood, too?"*

*Garfiel only replied with a snort.*

"*So the Sanctuary is a place where demihumans with mixed blood live?"
Subaru asked as Garfiel stood up from his perch, Otto looking in from
the driver's window with a curious expression.*

"*We welcome you, Emilia-sama, and your two escorts, too!" He made a
show for everyone to look ahead, with Otto grunting in surprise since he
was outside in the driver's area and could see first before anyone.*

*Subaru and Emilia leaned out of the windows of the moving cart.*

"*Roswaal likes to go around callin' it the Sanctuary, but it ain't the
kinda place that deserves such a nice name." Garfiel's narration of the
town was sinister and hopeless as Subaru observed ruined buildings with
moss and fungi overtake roads and fences alike. "Nothin' there but a
hodgepodge of halfwits."*

*A lot of people gathered around to look at the cart as Otto steered
Patrasche. All of them with animal hearts and tails, some even having
horns on their heads.*

"*It's just a dead-end testing ground."*

*People looked afraid as the carriage continued to roll through the town
to the point that Otto seemed uncomfortable. He quickly began looking
around. "Where do I..."*

*Suddenly, a stocking-covered foot walked its way infront of Patrasche,
with the owner of said foot speaking immediately.*

"*You're back. It took you long enough."*

*Subaru opened the driver's window immediately, gazing at the owner of
the voice with a wide smile.*

"*Ram!"*

"Sanctuary..." Crusch repeated the word, her voice dripping with irony.
She leaned forward, her eyes narrowing as the camera panned over a
crumbling stone wall covered in thick, sickly moss. "I have seen refugee
camps on the borders of the Empire that looked more hospitable than
this. Roswaal-sama, your definition of 'Sanctuary' is... illuminating."

Roswaal didn't answer. He sat perfectly still, his mismatched eyes fixed
on the ruins of the town he technically owned.

"Look at those houses," Naoko whispered, her hand flying to her mouth.
"Kenichi, the roofs are falling in. There's... is that mold growing on
the window frames?"

Kenichi's jaw was set tight. To him, this looked like the aftermath of a
natural disaster that everyone had simply given up on fixing. "It's a
slum." He looked at the screen, then at the sleeping Subaru. "Our son
spent weeks here? In this squalor?"

"It's not just the buildings," Ricardo rumbled from the back. The large
wolf-man's voice was uncharacteristically low, a dangerous growl
vibrating in his chest. "Look at the folks. Look at their eyes."

Mimi and Hetaro, usually so full of energy, were huddled close to
Ricardo's sides. They watched as the screen showed a group of
demihumans---children with floppy ears and scales, adults with
horns---watching the carriage with hollow, fearful expressions.

"They look... hungry," Mimi whispered, her usual cheer replaced by a
hauntingly quiet observation. "And sad. Why are they so sad?"

"Because they're 'half-bloods', Mimi," Tivey answered, his monocle
reflecting the gray light of the screen. "In the world outside, they're
shunned. And here, in the place that's supposed to protect them...
they're living in a graveyard. It's a ghetto, that's what it is."

Ricardo's fist slammed into his palm. "I've seen some bad shit in the
mercenary life, but callin' a dump like this a 'Sanctuary' is a spit in
the face to every demihuman tryin' to make a life. If this is how the
Mathers family 'protects' its people, then the Mathers family is
failin'."

"A testing ground," Julius noted, his pen scratching furiously against
his paper. "He called it a 'dead-end testing ground.' Not a town. Not a
refuge. A laboratory." He looked at Anastasia. "My lady, this is a
violation of the Kingdom's charters on the treatment of demi-human
populations. If this were public knowledge..."

"If it were public knowledge, the Council would've seized it years ago,"
Anastasia finished for him, her greed replaced by a sharp, political
disgust. "But it's a 'Witch's Tomb.' That's the shield Roswaal uses to
keep prying eyes out. He's hidin' a tragedy behind a legend."

"Don't listen to that blonde brat, in fact!" Beatrice snapped, though
her own eyes were fixed on the ruins with a hint of sorrow. "Mother...
Mother designed this place as a laboratory for the soul, I suppose. It
was never meant to be a town. That the people here have suffered is a
result of time and neglect, not just the barrier's nature!"

But the theater-goers couldn't shake the image of the ruined buildings
and the fearful eyes of the residents.

For the parents, it was a terrifying look at the world their son was
forced to survive in.

For the demihumans, it was a reminder of the cruelty of bloodlines.

And for the knights, it was the first piece of evidence that the
"Sanctuary" was a lie built on the bones of a Witch.

"Well," Anastasia leaned back, her eyes cold. "The 'Warden' wanted us to
see the truth. I think we're startin' to see the rot."

*The screen changes to show the maid standing tall with her arms
crossed.*

"*I haven't a clue which Barusu you are, but I'm disappointed by how
long it took you to get here. Ah, but I suppose I was a fool to expect
more of you." Ram put her hands on her hips while glaring at him.*

*Subaru had to show offense at this as he clenched his fists. "If your
story is that 'haven't a clue' who I am, stick to it!"*

"*Hmph." Ram huffed with a simple wave of her hand, as if to brush him
off.*

"Kind of nice to see someone treating him like he needs to be treated."
Kenichi grinned at the Ram on screen.

"Ram has her own charm when she's interacting with Subaru-kun." Rem
whispered to the parents with a proud smile on her face.

"I have a feeling that my sister is saying something really incorrect."
Ram sighed in the back, not being able to hear the parents and her
sister converse about her. Though her eyes turned back up to look at
Subaru with a slight smile.

*Once everyone's out of the carriage, Ram straightens her posture with
her hands moving infront of her. "Emilia-sama, I welcome you.
Roswaal-sama is waiting inside. Allow me to show you the way."*

*Ram turned around but paused to look behind Emilia. "Garf, take the
ground dragon and carriage somewhere suitable."*

"*Is that any way to treat me?" Garfiel looked bothered by the
disrespect in her tone. But it's quickly replaced with a smile. "That's
just how I like it, though."*

"Ew... That's gross." Petra commented with her tongue out.

"I can treat ya like that!" Mimi announces with a giggle while her
brothers just sputter and stutter apologies as Garfiel gets a red blush
on his face.

Ram had the grace not to comment and sighed at everyone as if they were
children. Which they were, for the record.

*He turned to Otto and waved at him. "Yo, cabbie, follow me."*

"*Are you referring to me?! That's the worst name you've called me yet!"
Otto's outrage could burn worlds as he is subject to more bullying.
"Also, I have to be alone with you?!"*

*With Emilia and Ram watching, Subaru gave Otto a thumbs up and a
supportive grin.*

"*I'll pick up what's left of you." He told Otto with his teeth shining
like the hero best friend he was destined to be.*

"*WHY AM I BEING TREATED SO ROUGHLY?!" Otto whined, but Subaru only
chuckled at him.*

"*What are you talking about? When I was unconscious, Emilia only stayed
safe because you were there." Subaru looked up at Otto and gave him a
grin. "I'm grateful."*

"*O-Oh?" The merchant clearly was awestruck at receiving such common
decency. "W-Well, as long as you're appreciative... Right." With that,
Otto drove the carriage away.*

*Subaru continued to look on with his lips forming a grin. "He's too
easy."*

"NATSUKI-SAAAAAAN!"

Otto's scream of pure, unadulterated betrayal ripped through the
theater, nearly drowning out the explosive laughter that followed. The
merchant was standing up, pointing a trembling finger at the screen as
if he could strike the animated Subaru down with his sheer indignation.

"You monster! You absolute demon!" Otto wailed, his face buried in his
hands as he sank back into his seat. "I was so moved! I felt so
appreciated! I was ready to follow you into the fire because of that
smile, and you were just... you were just playing me like a cheap lute!"

"Pwahahahaha!" Ricardo's laughter was like a physical blow, shaking the
seats of everyone in the row. "Oh, kid, you really are a soft touch!
'He's too easy!' I'm gonna be usin' that one for a week!"

"It's true, though!" Felt barked, her feet kicking the air in delight.
"Big Bro's got you on a leash, Cabbie! One nice word and you're waggin'
your tail!"

Even Kenichi and Naoko couldn't hold back. Wiping tears from his eyes,
he leaned against his wife.

"He really does take after me, doesn't he? That 'Hero Smile' is a lethal
weapon in the Natsuki household. Sorry, Otto-kun, you never stood a
chance."

Mimi and Hetaro were giggling uncontrollably, with Mimi even imitating
Subaru's thumbs-up toward a miserable-looking Otto.

However, amidst the cacophony of mirth, one corner of the theater
remained a tundra of silence.

Julius sat rigid, his hands clasped so tightly over his notebook that
the paper was beginning to crinkle. He wasn't laughing. In fact, his
eyes were fixed on Otto and Garfiel with a gaze that was cold, sharp,
and deeply wounded.

He remembered the earlier exchange---the way Garfiel had looked him in
the eye and told him to "fuck off" with a feral intensity that left no
room for negotiation. He remembered Otto's desperate, frantic wave to
stop any violence, not out of a desire for peace, but out of a need to
protect the secrets of the Emilia camp.

/Allies/, Julius thought, the word tasting like ash in his mouth.

"Is something wrong, Julius-san?" Tivey asked softly, noticing the
knight's pallor.

"Nothing," Julius replied, his voice a clipped, metallic rasp. He didn't
look at the small merchant. He didn't look at the screen. He kept his
eyes on the back of Otto's head. "I am merely realizing that the 'wall'
around the Sanctuary isn't just made of stone and magic. It is made of
people who would rather let their 'allies' stumble in the dark than
offer a single hand of truth."

*He looks back at the girls behind him, his eyes particularly showing a
serious focus on Ram.*

*The maid noticed immediately, putting her hands on her hips. "What is
it?"*

*Subaru's expression is replaced by an unsure smile. "Oh, just...
Wondering how you were doing." He glanced down with a small smile that
didn't reach his eyes. "You remember what was going on the last time I
saw you."*

*Flashbacks to Ram and Wilhelm informing Emilia to evacuate the mansion
before the cult's incursion on Roswaal's lands played, with Subaru
standing behind them in Emilia's appearance-concealing hoodie.*

"*I couldn't get in touch with you after that, and to be honest, I was
really worried."*

"Being worried about by Pal... Must be an experience, huh?" Al, for the
first time since a long while in the theater, had decided to remind Ram
of their friendship by checking in on her. "Are you really... just an
older sister in disguise?"

Ram scoffed, leaning away from Roswaal to wink at the helmet wearing man
in the same row as them. "Why must you bring such a horrifying link
between me and someone like Barusu? Absolutely vile."

*Ram held him with a small smile, putting a hand to her chest. "As you
can see, I am as lovely and healthy as ever." Ram's smile turned kind
and informed him with warmth about more. "The villagers of Arlam are all
safe, too."*

*Subaru's face looked like a thousand needles had suddenly disappeared
from beneath his feet at the news.*

"*You can be at ease." Ram encouraged him softly as relief flooded
through him.*

"She was really helping him relax? Subaru really found such amazing
people for his company." Naoko commented with her face beaming. "I can't
imagine to understand how he feels with such good friends. I'm so happy,
Kenichi... I'm so glad!"

"Honey... You look like you're about to cry!" Kenichi held on to his
wife with a hint of worry and amusement.

Rem smiled slightly at the parents with a sad tint to it.

"*I see." Subaru smiled widely... But the light didn't reach his eyes
when he suddenly looked down. "Yeah, that's... a real relief."*

*A flashback of Ram and Rem teasing Subaru played in his mind.*

"*That's a relief." He repeated, hollow, empty, and without looking Ram
in the eyes.*

*Ram seemed to notice that something was off and kept her gaze on him
with an inquiring eyebrow raise.*

*Subaru stuttered for a moment before looking away from her again.
"U-Um, listen..." he couldn't continue as he bit his lip, holding back a
lot of anguish that once it began, couldn't be stopped.*

"..." Rem looked at the floor, unable to help with any of Subaru's pain
as his struggle was shown to the entire theater.

"Man... What a doomed atmosphere we got right now..." Ricardo commented
with guilt as the beastman's ears drooped. His lofty grin seemed to
shrink into nothing.

"Oh..." Naoko's earlier excitement died as she remembered what had been
long forgotten for the people in the screen's world.

"Rem... He's the only one who remembers her and..." Emilia struggled to
continue her sentence.

"It's not like you have to say it out loud, in fact." Beatrice sighed,
her face dropping as well. "It's clear for all to see, I suppose."

"At least he'd done the job and told you in the end... He cared."
Roswaal commented with a bit of comfort, trying to do his part for Ram,
seeing that no one was attending to her.

But the pink-haired maid leaned away from his touch, crossing her arms.
"Barusu wouldn't have ever failed to do his duty... He didn't have a
book to anchor him."

*So... Emilia decided to call from his side. "Hey, Ram... This really
isn't a place to stand around and talk. Would you show us inside?"*

"*Certainly." Ram gave a polite bow to Emilia and turned toward the
house behind her, leading them both.*

*As Ram opened the door and entered, Emilia stepped towards Subaru with
a look of concern. "Subaru, are you all right?"*

"*Yeah." He grumbled with a look of distance at his shoes.*

"*I'm sorry for interrupting you, but..." Emilia gave him a guilty look,
unable to find the words.*

*Subaru shook his head. "No, I get it. I just freaked out a little. For
her to not even ask about Rem when all this is going on..." Subaru
smiled with the most fake, stretched smile he could muster. "I'm
pathetic, huh?"*

"Why does he always say that?" Emilia muttered with a tired sigh,
bringing her hand closer to his again so she can squeeze at it tightly.
"Can't he see how much it hurts to hear something like that from him?"

Most of the theater had gone quiet and uncomfortable by the notion of
Subaru's self esteem. His parents in particular found what they were
hearing appalling.

"He's apologizing for something that's not even his fault... I saw what
he did for the girl when she was found... We all did..." Naoko couldn't
but close her eyes. "This son of mine... He's so small in his mind."

"Only in his mind, honey." Kenichi kissed his wife on her head, not
wishing her to feel alone in her love for their son.

*Emilia's eyes hardened, and she moved immediately to clench her hands
around his, stepping closer to look him in the eye. "It's only natural
to be scared. I'll never think that's pathetic."*

"*Let's go," she said, giving him a smile of encouragement. "We'll fix
what happened to Rem and everything else, too."*

"*Got it." Subaru's eyes closed with his tension disappearing from his
shoulders as Emilia held his hand. "No matter what happens, I'll be your
meat shield and keep you safe."*

"What a different context that gives..." Felix didn't find the humor but
he had to give a fake chuckle anyway, tail switching chairs out of
discomfort. "... Forget I said that."

The healer noticed how everyone seemed uncomfortable by the idea,
especially the parents, who couldn't stomach the thought of how literal
that 'meat shield' comment might have been.

*The scene changes to the household in the Sanctuary, now encased in the
afterglow of the sunset as nighttime began.*

"*Ah, Emilia-sama and Subaru-kun. It feels like it's been so very long
since I last saw y~ooo~u." Roswaal's voice filled the screen as the
scene changed to show Subaru and Emilia standing in a room with shocked
faces.*

"*I was going to slug you when I saw you again, but now..." Subaru
mumbles while staring very openly toward Roswaal's form. (Pause) "What
the heck happened?"*

*Before Subaru and Emilia, the blue-haired mage was looking at them with
only his yellow eye open, bringing no shame to the rest of what his body
was revealing.*

*The man who sat before them was covered from the neck to the very end
of the torso with bandaging and wound covering treatments. A white
bandage was wrapped around his head, also.*

"*What could have hurt you so badly?" Emilia asked as Roswaal leaned
back on the bed where his body was lying.*

"Good grief..." Kenichi whispered, his hands tightening on the armrests.
"He looks like he went through a meat grinder. I thought you said this
guy was a big-shot lord? A powerful wizard?"

"He is," Wilhelm answered, his eyes narrowing as he leaned forward.
"Which is precisely why this sight is so disturbing. To see a mage of
his rank incapacitated to this degree... it implies an opponent or a
trial of overwhelming power."

"Ferris, what do you see?" Crusch asked, her voice low.

Felix didn't answer immediately. He was standing, his cat-like eyes
scanning every inch of the screen with clinical precision. "Those aren't
just cuts or bruises, Crusch-sama. Look at the way the bandages are
wrapped around his torso... they're thickest over his mana gate. And the
burns on his face---those aren't from a fire. That's magical backlash.
It's as if his own power turned inside out and tried to consume him,
nya."

"I do w~ooo~nder why you would be asking your knight about the extent of
my injuries when I'm sitting right h~eee~re with you all, hm?" Roswaal
hummed in his seat. "I don't appreciate being ignored."

"You gotta start learning how to be ignored then," Al snorted. "Because
no one in this room is trying to get answers from you."

"That is a little hurtful," Roswaal shook his head, though he kept his
eccentric smile. "Finally something of interest."

"*Well, now, where shall I beg~iii~n?" Roswaal smiled as he was about to
begin his yapping session before Subaru cut him off with all the
disrespect a Crip can give to a Blood like the Margrave.*

"*Hey, before that, just one thing... The alliance with Crusch-san is
on. Now, are you satisfied with the results you got from abandoning me?"
Subaru's glare foretold a lot.*

*Roswaal grinned without hiding his satisfaction. "Yes, most
satisf~iii~ed. Truly, truly, you were the once-in-a-lifetime windfall I
had long waited for."*

Many suspicious glances were given toward Roswaal from the entire Emilia
camp who kept to themselves and didn't share anything with anyone, and
the Anastasia camp who'd been planning on using every detail from what
they're learning as possible.

"Subaru was abandoned by this guy?" Kenichi noted beside his wife,
looking at the screen with a slight frown.

Julius tensed at the memory of what happened in the capital and kept his
focus on his notes to hide himself from the judgment of the parents
should that scene play before them.

*Subaru sighed as Emilia looked at him with increasing agitation. "Oh,
really?" He looked down, as if dealing with something inside his head.*

*Seeing him quiet, Emilia decided to continue for Subaru. "Roswaal," she
called softly from Subaru's side. "What is this place?"*

"*Since we got here... no, since we made contact with the barrier...
I've had this anxious feeling in my chest, and I can't relax." The
screen showed landscape shots of the deycribid villages of the
Sanctuary.*

"It's the mana density, I suppose," Beatrice noted, her drills unusually
still. "A place where a Witch's soul is anchored will always reject
those who do not belong. It is a house that has forgotten how to be a
home, in fact."

"*I've heard it called the 'Sanctuary,' but it doesn't seem like one to
me at all." Emilia's voice softens along with her eyes as she looks to
the floor. "In fact, it feels more like..."*

*When she herself grew quieter than Subaru, there was no one in the room
but Roswaal to finish her sentence, with his face adopting a most
sinister expression on his face with both his multi-colored eyes shining
a glint.*

"*The Witch's Graveyard?" Roswaal's voice was filled with warmth, which
increased the disturbing, obsessive nature of how he treated this
reveal. So much so that Emilia stepped back a bit.*

"*Does it seem to make far more sense to call it th~aaa~t?"*

"*What do you mean?" Emilia held her hands to her chest with a confused
yet worried frown.*

"*Exactly what I said, nothing more or less." Roswaal looked down at his
hand as his tone became more rueful. "This is where the Witch of Greed,
Echidna, met her end long ago, and in my mind, it is worth calling a
'Sanctuary.'"*

"Look at his face," Kenichi said, his voice hard. "He's not talking
about a cemetery. He's talking about a holy land. That's the look of a
man who's found his religion, and he's willing to burn everything else
to keep it."

"He's terrifying," Naoko added, her grip on Subaru's hand tightening.
"How can he call a graveyard a 'Sanctuary' with such a happy voice? It's
not right. Subaru... please don't listen to him."

"I do agree that this tone from the Margrave is more than...
worshipful." Crusch narrowed her eyes as her and Anastasia scrutinized
the man far more than anyone else in the room. While Felt and Priscilla
ignored Roswaal's existence as someone of worthless existence.

"*Echidna..." Subaru's face was struck with a weird form of shock,
staring off into space.*

"*Subaru? Are you all right?" Emilia asks him from the side.*

*Subaru swallowed and shook his head, waving his hand with an easy
smile. "Yeah, I'm fine."*

"He's choking on it," Al noted, his helmet tilting as he watched Subaru
struggle. "Can't say anything, Pal."

"So he was holding on to that meeting while in that moment with us?"
Emilia looked sympathetic toward Subaru, her eyes now far more knowing.

"This will make him more isolated than ever," Julius sighed, showing the
same worry Reinhard was feeling while watching this.

*He turns to Roswaal with a frown. "And why are you overseeing a place
with that kind of backstory?"*

"*It's quite simple," Roswaal answered with an easy smile. "This land
has been overseen by the heads of the Mathers family for generations."*

Julius's pen snapped in his hand. The sharp crack echoed in the
relatively quiet theater.

"Generations," the Finest of Knights whispered, his golden eyes
narrowing into dangerous slits as he stared at the back of the theater.
"The Mathers domain has harbored the soul of a Witch for generations...
and the Council was entirely blind to it."

"Or they chose to look the other way, nya," Felix added, his own ears
pinned back in clear distaste. "Either way, it stinks."

"*Then the Mathers family and the Witch of Greed have---" Emilia's
conclusion was cut short by a firm, almost a burst, of emotion from the
eccentric man standing before them.*

"*Echidna." He corrected.*

The sudden sharpness in his voice on the screen made several people in
the audience flinch. It wasn't just the interruption; it was the raw,
uncharacteristic reverence laced within that single word. It lacked his
usual sing-song drawl, replacing it with a deadly serious devotion.

"*Huh?" Emilia squeaked softly.*

*Roswaal's smile was easy and warm, unusually so, as he waved a bandaged
arm forward in a welcoming manner.*

"He... he interrupted Emilia-sama?" Rem blinked, her protective
instincts flaring. The sheer disrespect shown to a Royal Candidate was
jarring enough, but the reason for it was what truly unsettled the maid.
"Roswaal-sama... What have you done?"

"*Please, when you speak of her, do use her name. The name of 'Witch of
Greed' makes her sound so evil." He waved a finger at her. "That's not
very nice, is it?"*

"A Witch... He speaks of a Witch as if she were a maiden to be courted,"
Crusch said, her voice laced with absolute disgust. Even without
actively focusing, the wind reading around the Margrave in the back row
must have been a hurricane of twisted, sickening devotion.

In that back row, the real Roswaal didn't even attempt to defend his
on-screen counterpart. He simply closed his mismatched eyes, a serene,
genuinely fond smile gracing his painted lips.

"She is no mere Witch to me," Roswaal whispered into the dark, a
statement meant for no one but himself, though Ram heard it clearly. The
pink-haired maid simply tightened her grip on her own skirt, her eyes
downcast in quiet resignation.

"You really are a pathetic simp, Margrave."

The voice was dry, metallic, and devoid of any amusement. Everyone
turned to look at Al. The helmeted knight wasn't looking at Roswaal; his
gaze was fixed squarely on the screen, his one hand clenched into a
trembling fist upon his knee. "Defending her name like she gives a damn
about yours... Teacher really did a number on your head, didn't she?"

Priscilla didn't reprimand her knight. She merely observed the unfolding
drama from behind her fan, her red eyes gleaming with cruel
entertainment. "A fool dancing on the strings of a dead woman. How
delightfully tragic."

Kenichi swallowed hard, pulling Naoko a little closer. The warm,
fatherly demeanor he usually carried was replaced by a cold, protective
instinct. "Honey... I don't think that clown guy is just a weird boss
anymore."

"He looks..." Naoko shivered, rubbing her arms as she stared at the
screen. "He looks like one of those fanatics on the news back home. The
ones who smile right before they do something terrible for their cult."

Kenichi nodded grimly. "Yeah. He's got that exact look."

"*Um, then... the Mathers family and Echidna have been acquainted for a
long time, and that's why they've always overseen this place?" Emilia
asked with her hand lowering from her chest, but the other staying on
Puck's jewel.*

"Acquainted is putting it far too lightly, I suppose," Beatrice murmured
from Subaru's lap, her voice barely above a whisper. Her
butterfly-patterned eyes looked at Roswaal with a mixture of pity and
deep-seated disdain. "To be so thoroughly shackled by a past that Mother
herself would have already discarded... You truly are a pitiful man,
Roswaal."

"If this is true," Reinhard finally spoke up, his voice steady but
carrying the immense weight of the Sword Saint's authority, "then
Margrave Mathers, your actions border on high treason against the
Kingdom of Lugunica."

Julius nodded firmly in agreement, standing from his seat to look
directly at Roswaal. "I must agree. Hiding a Witch's grave, actively
revering her name, and admonishing a Royal Candidate for speaking ill of
a historical calamity... This cannot go unanswered once we return to the
capital."

Roswaal finally opened his eyes, glancing over his shoulder at the
assembled knights with an infuriatingly calm expression. "Treason, you
saaaaay? I merely maintain the lands passed down to me. What lies
beneath them is simply... family history."

"Family history my ass," Felt barked from the front, throwing her hands
up in the air. "You're a cultist in a clown suit! Big Bro's out here
dying on repeat, and he's working for a total lunatic!"

"*Yes, though to s~aaa~y we 'oversee' it is a bit of an exaggeration.
Echidna's barrier prevents entry by outsiders who fail to follow the
forest's proper procedure. In addition, the barrier has certain effects
on those whose blood meets certain conditions."*

Emilia flinched in her seat, her hand instinctively rising to her
forehead as the phantom pain of the barrier rejecting her half-elf blood
resurfaced. Next to her, Naoko noticed the gesture and gently placed a
comforting hand on the girl's shoulder, her motherly instincts flaring
up.

"He knew," Crusch stated, her voice slicing through the silence like a
drawn blade. Her amber eyes narrowed dangerously as she turned her head
to look at the back row. "You sent her in there fully aware of what the
barrier would do to her."

Roswaal didn't offer a defense, his mismatched eyes remaining fixed on
the screen with an infuriatingly calm disposition.

*A sinister bell was playing faintly in the background as Roswaal's eyes
turned to her.*

"*Did you not experience this yourself, Emilia-sama?"*

"You bastard," Garfiel snarled from his seat, his fangs bared. The
armrests of his chair groaned under the sudden, crushing grip of his
hands. "Playin' games with 'em while they don't know shit about what yer
really up to."

"Garf-san, please," Otto whispered frantically, though he didn't look
entirely opposed to the blonde's anger. The merchant's face was pale,
realizing just how manipulative their employer was truly being at this
exact moment in time.

*Emilia looked uncomfortable before Subaru interjected.*

"*And how did you end up in such a sorry state?"*

"Yeah, who kicked yer ass?" Felt barked a short, humorless laugh.
"Because whoever it was, I'd like to shake their hand. They didn't hit
ya nearly hard enough, in my opinion."

"Such vulgarity, yet I find myself inclined to agree with the
sentiment," Priscilla hid a cruel smirk behind her fan, her red eyes
gleaming with amusement at the Margrave's battered state on screen.

*Roswaal held a small smile and allowed Ram, who was standing beside
him, to speak to that.*

"*Did you not find it at all strange, Emilia-sama?" Ram spoke to the
half-elf.*

Anastasia sighed, rubbing the bridge of her nose beneath her scarf.
"Spirits above... they really are too pure for this world. Ya can
practically see the wool being pulled over their eyes."

"It is a painful level of trust," Julius agreed softly, pausing his
note-taking to look at the screen with a mixture of pity and
frustration. "To not even question why the refugees hadn't returned...
Natsuki-san and Emilia-sama rely too heavily on the assumed goodwill of
their allies."

"That ain't a flaw, Knight-boy," Al's gruff voice filtered through his
helmet. "It's just a damn shame they put that trust in a snake."

"*Huh?" She replied once again, clearly more confused.*

"*That all the people who fled the Sanctuary, as well as Roswaal-sama
and myself, stayed here and didn't return to the mansion?"*

"*Well..." Emilia looked to Subaru as he did to her, both having the
same curiosity clear on their faces.*

"*I mean, you stayed here because you're hurt, right?" Subaru put his
hands on his hips, looking at the injured man on the bed.*

In the audience, Kenichi let out a long, heavy sigh, letting his head
fall back against his seat.

"He's so dense sometimes," Kenichi muttered, a strained, loving
exasperation in his voice. "I taught him to look for the good in people,
but maybe I should have taught him to look for the trapdoors too."

"He's just trying to be optimistic, dear," Naoko whispered back, though
she too looked incredibly anxious at the corner her son was unknowingly
walking into.

*Roswaal's smile grew even more. "We are all being held under house
arrest here in the Sanctu~aaa~ry."*

"*Huh?!" Emilia and Subaru both let out in unison.*

"*Myself, Ram, and the people of Arlam... Oh," Roswaal closed his blue
eye while keeping the yellow one smiling at them both, "and you two as
well, from the moment you entered h~eee~re."*

"House arrest?!" Heinkel snorted in the back. "You lured your own damn
Royal Candidate into a hostage situation?!"

"This is treason," Reinhard stated, his voice devoid of its usual
warmth. The Sword Saint slowly turned his head to face Roswaal, his blue
eyes icy and unyielding. "To hold citizens of Arlam and a Royal
Candidate against their will, knowing full well they could not escape...
Margrave Mathers, your actions border on a declaration of war against
the Kingdom's laws."

"Is it treason if it occurs within my own priv~aaa~te domain?" Roswaal
finally spoke in the theater, his singsong tone dripping with
condescension. "They entered of their own free will, did they not?"

"They entered because you orchestrated it, you clown!" Crusch slammed
her fist onto her armrest, her usual composure completely shattering.
"You held the villagers hostage to force their hand!"

"I've done it to help release them." Roswaal's voice dropped the accent
for one second, which forced Reinhard and Crusch back as the screen
above continued to show what was hidden from the rest of them.

Garfiel turned from Otto's curious gaze.

"So that's it." Kenichi's voice wasn't loud, but it cut through the
shouting of the knights and candidates. The father sat rigid, his hands
balled into tight fists on his lap. He looked at the screen, at the
smiling, bruised face of the man who had just trapped his son.

"Kenichi..." Naoko whimpered, holding his arm tightly.

"He didn't just wander into a bad situation," Kenichi continued, his
voice trembling with a terrifying, suppressed paternal rage. He slowly
turned in his seat, locking eyes with the Margrave. There was no
innocence or playful fatherly demeanor left in him. "You put a leash on
my boy. You dragged him into this cage."

"A necessary piece for the board, Natsuki-san," Roswaal replied
smoothly, though Ram subtly shifted her stance, placing herself
marginally more between her master and the furious father.

"I don't care about your board," Kenichi practically growled, leaning
forward. "If you think you can just play games with my son's life---"

"He's already played it, Pops," Garfiel interrupted, his voice
surprisingly hollow. The blonde teen wasn't yelling anymore; he was just
staring at the screen with an empty look. "Cap'n's trapped.
Emilia-sama's trapped. And there ain't a damn thing any of 'em can do
about it without dyin'."

The harsh reminder of Subaru's deaths in the opening instantly smothered
the fiery outrage in the room, replacing it with a suffocating blanket
of dread.

Beatrice, who had remained perfectly silent throughout the entire
exchange, simply tightened her tiny grip on Subaru's jacket, burying her
face into his chest as the reality of his impending, inescapable
suffering settled over them all.

*Suddenly, the door to the room opened as Garfiel barged inside. "The
hell? You guys are still flappin' your gums in here?"*

In the theater, the audience Garfiel immediately slouched down in his
seat, pulling his collar up over his neck. "Aw, man... here it comes,"
he muttered, his earlier rage at Roswaal momentarily replaced by a deep,
burning embarrassment.

Otto couldn't help but give his blonde friend a deadpan stare. "Really,
Garfiel? Barging in like a thug? We were just talking about how much of
a threat you were acting like..."

"Shaddup, bro! M' just playin' the part!" Garfiel hissed, though his
cheeks were tinting pink.

"*House arrest... Then are Roswaal's injuries..." Emilia glared as she
turned to Garfiel with a hint of accusation in her eyes.*

"*Nope," Garfiel smirked to himself as he cut her off. "He got hurt
'cause he was rejected by the trail."*

"*Trail?" Subaru and Emilia looked even more baffled. "What does that
mean?"*

"Rejected by the trial..." Julius echoed the word from his seat, his pen
tapping against his notebook. He turned his sharp gaze toward the back
row. "So the Margrave attempted to conquer this graveyard himself and
was physically repelled. I assume this was no simple magical feedback,
considering the state of your body, Roswaal-sama."

Roswaal simply smiled, his mismatched eyes offering nothing but a
hollow, amused glint. "Such is the fate of those who overstep their
bounds, Julius-kun. The Sanctuary is quite... particular about its
guests."

"Speaking of particularities..." Tivey whispered to Julius. "How come
you're not aiming for his head no more?"

Julius eyed Otto's back for a second and then shook his head. "We need
all of the information we need."

"*You've seen what happens when you come in contact with the barrier,
right?" Garfiel gave an evil grin as he explained. "Mixed-bloods who go
through that and still come in here are never allowed to leave."*

"*Does that mean Emilia-tan too?!" Subaru looked at Emilia in shock as
she herself let out a small gasp.*

"*The barrier doesn't mean squat to most people. Doesn't that seem a
little unfair, Emilia-sama?" Garfiel crossed his arms and began talking
in a normal tone instead of his exaggerated evil one. "The only way to
break the barrier is to go through the graveyard's trial, but if anyone
who isn't a mixed-blood tries it..."*

*He paused as Roswaal smiled at him from the side, with Ram watching in
silence.*

*Garfiel's grin widened to show his sharp teeth. "All the villagers are
rounded up in one place. So long as they don't act up, we won't hurt
'em. They even get three meals and a nap every day!"*

The sheer tension that had been building in the theater popped like a
balloon against a needle.

For a second, nobody spoke. They merely processed the terms of this
supposedly brutal hostage situation.

Then, a loud, obnoxious snort echoed from the front row.

Felt threw her head back and began cackling, slapping her knee. "Three
meals?! And a nap?! Spirits preserve us, what a terrifying villain!
They're gonna sleep 'em to death!"

"Hey!" Garfiel shouted from his seat, his face completely flushed. "I
was tryin' to be intimidatin'!"
